一開始流量全為 0（整數）。殘餘容量是「容量減流量」或「流量」，都是整數；增廣路徑的瓶頸是路上殘餘容量的最小值，也是整數。每次增廣只加或減這個整數，所以流量一直保持整數。演算法結束時得到的最大流就是整數流。這就是用最大流解二分圖匹配時，能保證每條邊流量是 0 或 1 的原因。


@q mf3-27 | al-mst-flow#3 | 2 | 證明
二分圖 $(L,R)$ 加上源點 $s$（連到每個 $L$ 點）和匯點 $t$（每個 $R$ 點連過去），所有容量 1。說明整數最大流的值等於最大匹配的大小。
---
整數流中每條邊流量是 0 或 1。每個 $L$ 點最多流進 1，所以最多流出 1 條到 $R$；每個 $R$ 點最多流出 1 到 $t$，所以最多被 1 條流到。把流量為 1 的 $L$–$R$ 邊挑出來，正好是一個匹配，大小等於流的值。反過來，任何匹配都能變成同樣大小的流（沿 $s\to u\to v\to t$ 送 1）。兩者一一對應，最大值相同。


@q np1-31 | al-np#1 | 1 | 證明
說明為什麼 P ⊆ NP。
---
問題在 NP 的意思是：答案是「是」時，存在一個證據，能在多項式時間內驗證。如果問題在 P，驗證者根本不需要證據：直接用多項式時間的演算法把問題解出來，看答案是不是「是」就好。所以每個 P 的問題都在 NP。


@q np2-27 | al-np#2 | 2 | 證明
說明怎麼把「漢米頓迴路」歸約到這個問題：「給每兩個城市之間的距離和一個數 $k$，有沒有一條走遍所有城市各一次、再回到起點、總長 $\le k$ 的路線？」
---
給一個 $n$ 點的圖 $G$，建一個完全圖：原本有邊的兩點距離 1、沒有邊的距離 2，令 $k=n$。$G$ 有漢米頓迴路 ⇔ 完全圖有一條只用距離 1 的邊、總長 $n$ 的巡迴；任何用到距離 2 的巡迴總長都 $>n$。建構只要 $O(n^2)$，所以 TSP 至少跟漢米頓迴路一樣難，是 NP-hard。



@q dc2-29 | al-dc#2 | 2 | 反例
有人說：「Karatsuba 不管數字多短都比直式乘法快。」兩個 2 位數相乘，比較兩者的運算量：
[x] 直式要 4 次個位數乘法；Karatsuba 要 3 次乘法但多了好幾次加減和拆數、拼回去的工作，不會比較快
[ ] Karatsuba 只要 1 次乘法
[ ] 他是對的，永遠比較快
[ ] 直式要 8 次乘法
---
Karatsuba 用多做加減換少做一次乘法，好處要在數字夠長、遞迴很多層時才顯現（$n^{1.58}$ vs. $n^2$）。實作上數字短於某個門檻（例如幾十位）時就改回直式乘法。


@q dc4-31 | al-dc#4 | 2 | 反例
Swapping two **adjacent** numbers changes the number of inversions by exactly 1. Someone says the same holds for swapping **any** two numbers. Check by swapping 3 and 1 in $[3,2,1]$. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The number of inversions drops from 3 to 0.
[x] Swapping non-adjacent numbers can change the number of inversions by more than 1.
[x] Any sort that only swaps adjacent numbers needs at least as many swaps as there are inversions.
[ ] The number of inversions drops from 3 to 2.
[ ] The number of inversions stays the same.
---
$[3,2,1]$ 有 (3,2)、(3,1)、(2,1) 共 3 對；交換後 $[1,2,3]$ 一對都沒有，「The number of inversions drops from 3 to 0.」、「Swapping non-adjacent numbers can…」對、「The number of inversions drops from 3 to 2.」、「The number of inversions stays…」錯。不相鄰的交換會同時改變中間那些數和兩端的關係。相鄰交換一次只消掉一個，所以插入、氣泡這類排序至少要做「逆序對個數」次交換，「Any sort that only…」對。


@q dp3-31 | al-dp#3 | 3 | 反例
0/1 背包只用一列 `dp[j]`（容量 $j$ 的最大價值）。有人把更新寫成 $j$ 從小到大：`dp[j] = max(dp[j], dp[j - w] + v)`。容量 2、只有一個物品（重 1、價值 1），結果 `dp[2]` 是多少？正確答案是多少？
== 2,1
?? 他的dp[2],正確答案
---
從小到大更新時，`dp[1]` 先被改成 1（用了物品），算 `dp[2]` 時用到的 `dp[1]` 已經包含這個物品，又加一次，變成用了兩次。0/1 背包要 $j$ 從大到小，確保 `dp[j - w]` 還是「沒考慮這個物品」的舊值。從小到大其實是「每種物品無限多個」的背包。


@q dp4-27 | al-dp#4 | 2 | 反例
Use a full weight-limit-table operation model with one basic operation per item-weight-limit pair. For n=30 and W equal to two to the power 60, compare the table with enumeration of all subsets. A separate trial uses n=1000 and W=1000. The operation budget is 10000000000 for each independent trial, and all inputs are binary-encoded. Do not infer feasibility merely from whether a formula contains a product rather than an power expression. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The table would have $30\times2^{60}$ entries, far too many to fill.
[x] $W$ can be written with 61 bits.
[ ] $O(nW)$ is polynomial in the length of the input.
[x] Trying all $2^{30}$ subsets takes far fewer steps than filling that table.
[ ] With 1000 items and $W=1000$, the DP is also impractical.
---
「$30\times2^{60}$ 格」對：約 $3.5\times10^{19}$。

「61 位元」對：輸入長度很短，但 $W$ 本身的值是指數級。

「對輸入長度是多項式」錯：這就是「虛擬多項式」。

「試 $2^{30}$ 種比較少」對：約 $10^9$，再用折半列舉更少。

「1000 件、$W=1000$ 也不行」錯：$10^6$ 格，一下就算完。


@q dp5-30 | al-dp#5 | 2 | 反例
A proposed LIS formula counts adjacent increases and adds 1. Test it on the nonempty sequences 3,4,1,2 and 5,6,1,2,3, comparing each result with a genuine increasing subsequence. Separately interpret the selection rule on 1,3,2,4 as selecting only entries that exceed their immediate predecessor, with no automatically included first entry. A final test uses 4,3,2,1. Distinguish adjacent comparisons from choices allowed to skip positions. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The sequence $3,4,1,2$ is a counterexample to the claim.
[ ] For $1,3,2,4$, taking every element that is larger than the element just before it gives a longest increasing subsequence.
[x] The sequence $5,6,1,2,3$ is a counterexample to the claim.
[x] The claimed formula is never smaller than the true LIS length.
[ ] The LIS length of $4,3,2,1$ is 0.
---
「$3,4,1,2$ 是反例」對：兩次上升，公式給 3，但 LIS 只有 2（3,4 或 1,2）：兩段上升之間掉下去了，不能接起來。

「$1,3,2,4$ 挑比前一個大的就是 LIS」錯：挑出來的是 3、4，長度 2；真正的 LIS 是 1,2,4 或 1,3,4，長度 3。（這個序列的公式值 3 倒是剛好對。）

「$5,6,1,2,3$ 是反例」對：公式 4，LIS 是 3（1,2,3）。

「公式永遠不會比 LIS 小」對：LIS 中相鄰兩個 $a_p<a_q$ 之間一定有一次相鄰上升，而且不同的間隔用到不同的上升，因此上升次數 $\ge$ LIS $-1$（程式窮舉 7 個數以內的排列確認）。

「$4,3,2,1$ 的 LIS 是 0」錯：任何一個數本身就是長度 1 的遞增子序列。


@q gd3-29 | al-greedy#3 | 3 | 反例
Someone says: "In a Huffman code, the most frequent character always gets a codeword of length 1." Which of the following frequency distributions are counterexamples?
[x] Four characters, each with frequency 25%
[x] Five characters, each with frequency 20%
[ ] One character at 90% and two others at 5% each
[ ] Only two characters
[ ] One character at 60% and three others at about 13% each
---
四個一樣時，Huffman 兩兩合併成兩組再合成根，每個碼 2 位元；五個 20% 時碼長是 2、2、2、3、3，也沒有 1 位元，兩個都是反例。90%、60% 那兩組最後一步都是大的字元自己和其他合併的結果配對，得到 1 位元；只有兩個字元時兩個都是 1 位元，都不是反例（程式跑 Huffman 確認）。出現最多的字元碼最短（或並列最短），但不一定是 1 位元。


@q mf1-33 | al-mst-flow#1 | 2 | 反例
A city connects 4 districts, $a$, $b$, $c$, and $d$, with roads whose costs are given below; the same network is also used for driving, where the cost of a road is its travel time in minutes:

| road | $a$–$b$ | $b$–$c$ | $a$–$c$ | $c$–$d$ | $b$–$d$ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| cost | 2 | 2 | 3 | 1 | 4 |

The planners first build a minimum spanning tree to decide which roads to pave. A councillor then claims: "The path between two districts in a minimum spanning tree is a path of minimum total weight between them in the whole network, so drivers lose nothing if only the paved roads are used." Two engineers check the claim, one with Kruskal's algorithm, breaking ties alphabetically, and one by computing shortest paths from $a$ to the other 3 districts, and they compare the two results road by road. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The minimum spanning tree of $G$ has total weight 5.
[x] In the minimum spanning tree, the path from $a$ to $c$ has weight 4, while the lightest path from $a$ to $c$ in $G$ has weight 3.
[x] Kruskal's algorithm, breaking ties alphabetically, adds the edges in the order $c$–$d$, $a$–$b$, $b$–$c$.
[ ] Because two edges have the same weight, $G$ has more than one minimum spanning tree.
[ ] The tree formed by the lightest paths from $a$ to all other vertices is also a minimum spanning tree of $G$.
---
「MST 總重 5」對：Kruskal 依權重取 $cd$(1)、$ab$(2)、$bc$(2)，三條就連通四個點，$1+2+2=5$。

「樹上 $a\to c$ 是 4、原圖最短 3」對：樹上要走 $a$–$b$–$c$，$2+2=4$；原圖直接走 $a$–$c$ 只要 3。這就是說法的反例。

「Kruskal 順序 $cd$、$ab$、$bc$」對：$ab$、$bc$ 同為 2，照字母 $ab$ 先；兩條都不形成環，都收下。

「有相同權重所以 MST 不唯一」錯：權重相同只是「可能」不唯一。這裡兩條權重 2 的邊都必須選，換成 $ac$(3) 或 $bd$(4) 都更重，所以 MST 唯一。

「從 $a$ 出發的最短路徑樹也是 MST」錯：最短路徑樹是 $ab$、$ac$、$cd$，總重 6，比 5 大。MST 讓「所有邊加起來」最小，最短路徑樹讓「從起點到每個點」最短，目標不同。


@q np2-28 | al-np#2 | 3 | 反例
Someone claims: "If an NP-complete problem $A$ reduces to a problem $B$ ($A\le_p B$), then $B$ must be in NP." To test the claim, a student considers the halting problem, for programs of up to 1000 lines, which asks whether a given program halts on a given input and which no algorithm can decide at all. The student recalls that a polynomial-time reduction maps every instance of $A$ to an instance of $B$ with the same answer, so any algorithm for $B$ yields one for $A$ with only 1 extra polynomial-time step, about $n^2$ operations here. The student wants to state exactly what $A\le_p B$ proves about $B$, what else is needed to show that $B$ is NP-complete, and what would follow for $A$ if $B$ turned out to be solvable in polynomial time. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The reduction shows only that $B$ is NP-hard.
[x] $B$ may be harder and not in NP at all; the halting problem is an example.
[x] To prove that $B$ is NP-complete, one must also show that its yes-instances can be verified in polynomial time.
[x] If $B$ were in P, then $A$ would be in P too.
[ ] The reduction shows that $A$ is harder than $B$.
---
$A\le_pB$「只說明 $B$ 是 NP-hard」，$B$ 會解 $A$ 就會解，所以「$B$ 在 P 則 $A$ 也在 P」。$B$「可能更難、根本不在 NP」，例如停機問題；要證 NP-complete 還得「證明能快速驗證」。歸約說的是 $B$ 至少跟 $A$ 一樣難，不是 $A$ 比較難。


@q rc2-34 | al-recurrence#2 | 2 | 反例
有人想用 Master 定理解 $T(n)=2^nT(n/2)+n^n$。為什麼不能用？
[x] Master 定理要求 $a$ 是常數；這裡子問題的個數 $2^n$ 隨 $n$ 改變
[ ] 因為 $b=2$
[ ] 因為 $f(n)$ 是多項式
[ ] 可以用，答案是 $\Theta(n^n)$
---
Master 定理的形式是 $T(n)=aT(n/b)+f(n)$，$a\ge1$、$b>1$ 都是常數。$a$、$b$ 不是常數，或子問題大小不一樣（例如 $T(n/3)+T(2n/3)$）時，都得改用遞迴樹或代入法。


@q rc3-28 | al-recurrence#3 | 3 | 反例
In an ideal recursion tree for $T(n)=T(n/3)+T(2n/3)+n$, stop when size is at most one. Start at n equal to nine and measure path length by recursive reductions. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Repeatedly taking the one-third child reaches the base after two reductions.
[x] Repeatedly taking the two-thirds child reaches the base after six reductions.
[x] For growing n, both extreme path lengths are $\Theta(\lg n)$ despite differing constants.
[ ] Every root-to-terminal path has length two at n equal to nine.
[ ] The longest path always comes from repeatedly choosing the smaller one-third child.
---
「短兩次」對：9→3→1。「長六次」對：9×(2/3)⁵＝32/27 尚大於一，再乘 2/3 才小於一。「同對數階」對：分別約 log₃n、log₍₃/₂₎n，換底只差常數。「全部兩次」錯：反例已給六次路徑。「選較小反最長」錯：較小子問題縮得更快，應是最短路徑。


@q sp5-99 | al-sp#5 | 1 | 反例
有人說：「Floyd–Warshall 跟 Dijkstra 一樣，不能用在有負權邊的圖。」為什麼不對？
[x] Floyd–Warshall 只要沒有負環，負權邊也能正確處理；Dijkstra 才不行
[ ] 他是對的
[ ] Floyd–Warshall 只能用在無權圖
[ ] Floyd–Warshall 連有負環都能處理
---
Floyd–Warshall 是 DP，考慮「中間點只用前 $k$ 個」的所有情況，不依賴「先確定的點不會再變短」這個假設。有負環時最短路徑本身沒有定義，但可以用 $d[i][i]<0$ 偵測出來。



@q dc2-30 | al-dc#2 | 3 | 找錯
Let $x=a\cdot10^{m}+b$ and $y=c\cdot10^{m}+d$. A student computes $ac$, $bd$ and $(a+b)(c+d)-ac-bd$ as in Karatsuba, and then writes $xy=ac\cdot10^{2m}+(ad+bc)\cdot10^{2m}+bd$. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The middle coefficient must carry weight $10^m$, not $10^{2m}$.
[x] The expression $(a+b)(c+d)-ac-bd$ equals $ad+bc$.
[ ] The leading coefficient $ac$ should carry weight $10^m$.
[x] The low product bd has positional weight one.
[x] For m one and blocks a one, b two, c three, d four, the correct product is 408.
---

@q dc3-27 | al-dc#3 | 2 | 找錯
In the closest-pair combine step, the halves give minimum distance $\delta$ and the dividing line is $x=x_0$. A student checks only the points with $x_0\le x<x_0+\delta$ (the strip on the right side). Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] A crossing pair closer than $\delta$ has each endpoint less than $\delta$ from the dividing line.
[x] The standard symmetric strip has width $2\delta$.
[x] After sorting the strip by y, the standard packing proof permits checking at most seven following points per point.
[x] Keeping only right-side points can omit an endpoint needed for a closer crossing pair.
[ ] A symmetric half-width of $\delta/2$ always includes every crossing pair closer than $\delta$.
---

@q dc5-28 | al-dc#5 | 2 | 找錯
FFT is used to multiply two polynomials of degree 3. A student says: "Each polynomial has 4 coefficients, so evaluating each at 4 points is enough." Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The product has degree 6 and 7 coefficients, so at least 7 points are needed to recover it.
[x] In practice FFT pads to 8 points.
[x] With only 4 points, interpolation returns a polynomial of degree at most 3, not the product.
[ ] The student is right: 4 points suffice.
[ ] At least 16 points are required.
---
點值表示的乘法是同一點的值相乘，但結果的次數變高了，點數必須夠還原「結果」：6 次有 7 個係數，至少 7 個點，「The product has degree…」對、「4 points suffice」錯。FFT 要 2 的冪次，補零到 8 個點，「In practice FFT pads…」對；不需要 16。只取 4 個點，還原出來的是次數 ≤3 的多項式，和正確乘積不同，「With only 4 points,…」對。


@q dp2-35 | al-dp#2 | 2 | 找錯
For the LCS table $L[i][j]$ of strings $A$ and $B$, where $L[i][j]$ is the LCS length of the first $i$ characters of $A$ and the first $j$ characters of $B$, a student writes this recurrence:
```
L[i][0] = L[0][j] = 0
L[i][j] = L[i-1][j] + 1                 if a_i == b_j
L[i][j] = max(L[i-1][j], L[i][j-1])     otherwise
```
The student's program gives the right answer when both strings are `ab`, but it fails when $A=$ `aa` and $B=$ `a`. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] For equal last characters, use $L[i][j]=L[i-1][j-1]+1$.
[x] The incorrect rule returns two for strings aa and a, whose LCS length is one.
[x] The LCS length of any string and the empty string is zero.
[x] For unequal last characters, use $\max(L[i-1][j],L[i][j-1])$.
[ ] $L[i][0]=1$ for every positive i.
---
「相等時退兩邊再加一」對：配對一次需用掉兩個字元。「錯算二」對：aa 對 a 只有一個可配，錯式重用同一字元。「空字串長度零」對：沒有任何字元可配。「不相等取兩側最大」對：捨去其中一個末字元，考慮兩種可能。「邊界設一」錯：空字串對應整列或整欄都應零。

An input file contains n item records and a binary weight-limit W without leading zeros. In the numerical example n=10 and W=1000000000000000000, so the weight-limit integer entry occupies only 60 bits. The solver nevertheless allocates a column for every integer weight-limit. Distinguish bits in the file from the number represented by those bits, including the item fields when measuring the complete input. Which statements about the proposed n-plus-W size argument are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The weight-limit integer entry uses logarithmically many bits in W; numeric W is not its written length.
[ ] Ordinary binary input contains one symbol for each of the W weight-limit units.
[x] The example needs 60 weight-limit bits but about 1000000000000000000 table columns.

@q dp5-31 | al-dp#5 | 3 | 找錯
求**嚴格**遞增的 LIS，同學用 tail 陣列，每來一個數 $x$，找「第一個**大於** $x$ 的位置」取代，找不到就接在後面。對序列 2, 2，他得到多少？正確是多少？
== 2,1
?? 他的答案,正確答案
---
第二個 2 進來時，tail 是 `[2]`，沒有元素大於 2，被接在後面，變成 `[2,2]`，長度 2。但嚴格遞增不能有兩個 2。嚴格遞增要找「第一個**大於或等於** $x$」的位置；找「大於」的版本算的是非遞減子序列。

[x] The optimum takes all of $y$ and $z$, for total value 90.
[ ] The student's total value 60 is optimal.
[ ] Taking the lightest item first is the correct rule.

---

@q gd5-27 | al-greedy#5 | 3 | 找錯
A classmate says: "The matchings of a graph (edge sets in which no two edges share an endpoint) form a matroid, so greedily taking the heaviest edges yields a maximum-weight matching." Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Matchings need not satisfy the matroid exchange property.
[x] On the four-vertex path, $\{bc\}$ cannot be enlarged using either edge of matching $\{ab,cd\}$.
[x] With bc weight three and ab,cd each weight two, largest-weight-first obtains three rather than the optimum four.
[ ] A subset of a matching can contain two edges sharing an endpoint even when no pair in the original matching shares one.
[x] Restricting the graph to a path does not by itself restore augmentation for its matching family.
---

@q mf3-28 | al-mst-flow#3 | 3 | 找錯
To find a maximum matching in the bipartite graph with left vertices $u_1, u_2, u_3$, right vertices $v_1, v_2$, and edges $u_1$–$v_1$, $u_2$–$v_1$, $u_3$–$v_1$, $u_3$–$v_2$, we add a source $s$ with an edge to each $u_i$, a sink $t$ with an edge from each $v_j$, and direct every original edge from left to right. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] If each edge out of $s$ and each edge into $t$ can carry at most 1 unit, the maximum flow is 2, which equals the size of a maximum matching.
[x] If every edge can carry an unlimited amount, the maximum flow is unbounded, so it says nothing about matchings.
[ ] If each edge out of $s$ can carry at most 1 unit but the edges into $t$ are unlimited, the maximum flow is still 2.
[x] The left-to-right edges may carry at most 1 unit or be unlimited; either choice gives the same maximum flow value when the edges at $s$ and $t$ carry at most 1 unit.
[ ] Every maximum flow of value 2 must send flow along $u_2 \to v_1$.
---
「$s$、$t$ 的邊限 1 時最大流 2」對：$v_1$、$v_2$ 各只能收 1，最多 2；$u_1\to v_1$、$u_3\to v_2$ 達到 2。整數流中 $L\to R$ 有流量的邊就是一組匹配。

「全部不限量時最大流無限大」對：$s\to u_1\to v_1\to t$ 可以送任意多。

「只限 $s$ 端、$t$ 端不限仍是 2」錯：$u_1$、$u_2$、$u_3$ 各送 1 到 $v_1$ 或 $v_2$，流量 3，等於讓 $v_1$ 配給兩個人。$s$ 端限制「每個左邊點最多配一個」，$t$ 端限制「每個右邊點最多配一個」，兩邊都要。

「中間的邊限 1 或不限都可以」對：每個左邊點最多流進 1，流出去自然不超過 1。

「一定經過 $u_2\to v_1$」錯：$u_1\to v_1$、$u_3\to v_2$ 就是一組不用它的最大流。


@q np1-32 | al-np#1 | 1 | 找錯
A classmate says: "To prove that a problem is in NP, you must give a polynomial-time algorithm that solves it." As an example, the classmate considers deciding whether a road network of $n$ towns has a round trip that visits every town exactly once, for which no polynomial-time algorithm is known. For a yes-instance with $n = 50$, someone can show an ordering of the 50 towns, and we can check its 50 roads in $O(n)$ time, but nobody knows how to find such an ordering quickly. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] To show membership in NP, it suffices to verify a given certificate in polynomial time.
[ ] The classmate is right: membership in NP requires a polynomial-time algorithm that solves the problem.
[ ] One must prove that the problem has no polynomial-time algorithm.
[ ] A problem is in NP once a certificate can be found in polynomial time.
[ ] Membership in NP requires short certificates for both yes-instances and no-instances.
---
在 NP「只要能在多項式時間驗證給定的證據」；給出解法是證明在 P。不需要證明沒有快速解法；證據是給的，不必自己找得到；只有「是」的答案需要短證據，「否」也要的是 NP ∩ co-NP。


@q np2-29 | al-np#2 | 1 | 找錯
同學想證明新問題 $X$ 是 NP-hard，於是寫了一個把 $X$ 轉成 SAT 的多項式時間方法。錯在哪？
[x] 方向反了：$X\le_p$ SAT 只說明 $X$ 不比 SAT 難；要證明 $X$ 夠難，必須把已知的 NP-hard 問題（例如 SAT）歸約到 $X$
[ ] 他是對的
[ ] 要把 $X$ 歸約到 P 裡的問題
[ ] 要寫出指數時間的演算法
---
$A\le_pB$：能解 $B$ 就能解 $A$，所以 $B$ 至少跟 $A$ 一樣難。要讓 $X$ 當「至少一樣難」的那一方，它必須在右邊。

@q pb1-30 | al-prob#1 | 1 | 找錯
A fair coin is tossed 10 times, and a student wants the expected number of heads. The student lets $X_i=1$ if toss $i$ is heads and $X_i=0$ otherwise, so the number of heads is $X = X_1 + \cdots + X_{10}$, but then writes $E[X_i]=1$ "because $X_i$ is an indicator" and concludes that the expected number of heads is 10. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The mistake is $E[X_i]=1$.
[x] The expectation of an indicator equals the probability of its event.
[x] $E[X_i]=\frac12$, so the expected number of heads is 5.
[x] The same method would work even if the tosses were dependent.
[ ] Indicator variables cannot be used here, because the tosses are independent.
---
「錯在 $E[X_i]=1$」：$E[X_i]=1\cdot P(\text{正面})+0\cdot P(\text{反面})=\frac12$，「指示變數的期望＝機率」，10 次加起來「是 5」。線性不需要獨立，「投擲相關也照樣可以用」；獨立更不會妨礙使用。

@q rc4-30 | al-recurrence#4 | 2 | 找錯
$T(n)=T(\sqrt n)+1$。同學令 $m=\lg n$ 得 $S(m)=S(m/2)+1$，解出 $S(m)=\Theta(\lg m)$，最後寫 $T(n)=\Theta(\lg n)$。錯在哪？
[x] 換回來時 $m=\lg n$，所以 $T(n)=S(\lg n)=\Theta(\lg\lg n)$
[ ] 他是對的
[ ] 應該是 $\Theta(\sqrt n)$
[ ] 應該是 $\Theta(n)$
---
最後一步最容易忘：$S$ 的參數是 $m$，答案 $\lg m$ 要把 $m$ 換回 $\lg n$。直觀上 $n$ 一直開根號，要開 $\lg\lg n$ 次才會降到常數。


@q sp1-26 | al-sp#1 | 1 | 找錯

```
if (d[v] > d[u] + w) d[u] = d[v] + w;
```
and tests it with $d[u] = 2$, $d[v] = 10$, and $w = 3$, expecting $d[v]$ to become 5. Instead, after the call, $d[v]$ is still 10 and $d[u]$ has become 13. The teaching assistant asks the student to explain what relaxation is supposed to do and which part of the line is wrong, and to give the values of $d[u]$ and $d[v]$ after a correct relaxation. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The assignment should update d[v] to d[u]+w when the condition holds.
[x] Relaxing u-to-v uses the estimate at u to attempt improving the estimate at v.
[x] For d[u]=2, d[v]=10 and w=3, correct relaxation sets d[v] to five.
[ ] The improvement test should use d[v] less than d[u]+w rather than greater.
[x] The shown incorrect assignment does not lower d[v] because it assigns only to d[u].
---
「更新 v」對：候選路徑經 u 再走該邊，長度為 d[u]＋w。「用 u 改善 v」對：來源到 u 的估計提供新候選。「原估計十可改五」對：二加三為五，優於原來十。「判斷改小於」錯：只有原估計更大才需降低，反向會偏向更差候選。「錯式不降 v」對：原程式只寫 u，v 的值完全沒被指定；還可能把 u 的估計變差。


@q sp2-32 | al-sp#2 | 3 | 找錯
同學寫 Dijkstra 時，像 BFS 一樣在點**第一次被發現**（第一次被鬆弛到）時就標記為完成、之後不再更新。用 $s\to a$（5）、$s\to b$（1）、$b\to a$（1）檢查 $a$ 的距離：
[x] 先處理 $s$ 時 $a$ 被發現、距離 5 就被定死；但經過 $b$ 只要 2。要等點從優先佇列被**取出**時才算完成
[ ] 得到 2，他是對的
[ ] 得到 1
[ ] 得到 6
---
Dijkstra 的正確性靠「取出時一定是目前最小的距離，之後不可能再變小」。第一次發現時的距離只是暫時的上界。


@q sp3-29 | al-sp#3 | 2 | 找錯
Bellman–Ford is run on a graph with $V=5$ vertices and no negative-weight cycle. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] $V-1=4$ rounds of relaxing every edge always suffice.
[ ] 3 rounds always suffice, because by then every edge has been relaxed three times.
[x] After $k$ rounds, every vertex whose shortest path uses at most $k$ edges has its correct distance.
[x] If some round changes no distance, the algorithm may stop early.
[ ] If a 5th round still changes some distance, the edges were simply relaxed in a bad order.
---
「4 輪一定夠」對：沒有負環時最短路徑是簡單路徑，最多 $V-1=4$ 條邊。

「3 輪就夠」錯：邊的處理順序不好時，每一輪只保證多確定一條邊；一條 4 條邊的最短路徑要 4 輪才保證。

「$k$ 輪後，最多 $k$ 條邊的都正確」對：用歸納法，第 $k$ 輪會鬆弛到路徑上的第 $k$ 條邊。

「某輪沒更新就可提早結束」對：之後每一輪也不會再變。

「第 5 輪還在變是順序不好」錯：不管順序，4 輪後就該全部正確；第 $V$ 輪還能更新，代表從起點走得到負環。



@q dc1-30 | al-dc#1 | 2 | 情境
10 TB of data, too large for one computer, must be sorted. The common approach splits it into many chunks, lets many computers each sort some chunks, and then merges the sorted chunks. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] This is the divide-and-conquer idea: split into small problems, solve each, then combine.
[x] Merging the sorted chunks only needs to read each chunk sequentially.
[ ] The data should be loaded into memory all at once and sorted.
[ ] Sorting a random sample of the data is enough.
[ ] Inserting the records one by one into a single sorted list is the standard approach.
---
切成小問題、各自解決、再合併，就是分治；合併排序的結構天生適合分散，每一塊獨立處理，合併只要循序讀各塊（多路合併），「This is the divide-and-conquer…」、「Merging the sorted chunks…」對。10 TB 放不進記憶體；抽樣排序不是把全部排好；一筆一筆插入太慢，也無法分散。


@q dc2-31 | al-dc#2 | 2 | 情境
Cryptography often multiplies two integers of over 600 decimal digits. Each number is split into 64 blocks; schoolbook multiplication needs about $n^2$ block multiplications and Karatsuba about $n^{1.585}$, with $n=64$. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Schoolbook multiplication needs about $64^2=4096$ block multiplications.
[x] Karatsuba needs about $64^{1.585}\approx730$ block multiplications.
[x] The difference is about 5 to 6 times, and it grows as the numbers get longer.
[ ] The difference is about 64 times.
[ ] The difference is about 4096 times.
---
$64^{1.585}=2^{6\times1.585}\approx2^{9.5}\approx730$，比值 $4096/730\approx5.6$，「Schoolbook multiplication needs about…」、「Karatsuba needs about $64^{1.585}\approx730$…」、「The difference is about 5…」對，64 倍、4096 倍都錯。位數越長差距越大，這就是密碼學函式庫在大數乘法上使用 Karatsuba（或更進階方法）的原因。


@q dc3-28 | al-dc#3 | 2 | 情境
An air traffic system must quickly find the two closest aircraft among $n = 10^5$ aircraft, using either a method that compares every pair or the divide-and-conquer closest-pair algorithm. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Comparing every pair needs about $5\times10^9$ distance computations.
[x] Divide and conquer needs work on the order of $n\lg n\approx1.7\times10^6$.
[ ] Both approaches need about the same amount of work.
[ ] Divide and conquer needs more distance computations than comparing every pair.
[ ] Doubling the number of aircraft would roughly double the work of comparing every pair.
---
「每對都比約 $5\times10^9$」對：$n(n-1)/2\approx5\times10^9$。

「分治約 $1.7\times10^6$」對：$10^5\times\lg10^5\approx10^5\times17$。

「差不多」錯、「分治比較多」錯：差了約 3000 倍。在需要即時更新的系統裡差別非常大。

「飛機加倍，兩兩比較約加倍」錯：工作量和 $n^2$ 成正比，加倍後約變 4 倍。


@q dc5-29 | al-dc#5 | 2 | 情境
Two polynomials with $n = 10^5$ terms each are multiplied, as in a signal-processing convolution, either directly term by term or with the FFT. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Direct multiplication needs about $10^{10}$ multiplications.
[x] FFT needs work on the order of $n\lg n$, a few million operations.
[ ] Both approaches need about the same amount of work.
[ ] The FFT method multiplies the polynomials without ever converting them away from coefficient form.
[ ] Doubling the number of terms would roughly double the work of direct multiplication.
---
「直接乘約 $10^{10}$」對：每一項配每一項，$(10^5)^2$。

「FFT 約 $n\lg n$」對：轉成點值、逐點相乘、再轉回來，每步 $O(n\lg n)$，幾百萬的量級。

「差不多」錯：差了幾千倍。音訊濾波、影像模糊、大數乘法都靠這個。

「不離開係數形式」錯：FFT 的做法正是先轉成點值、在點值上逐點相乘，再轉回係數。

「項數加倍，直接乘約加倍」錯：工作量是 $n^2$，加倍後約 4 倍。


@q dp1-33 | al-dp#1 | 1 | 情境
爬 50 階樓梯（每次 1 或 2 階）的走法數，直接用 `f(n) = f(n-1) + f(n-2)` 遞迴算要非常久，加上「算過的存起來」幾乎瞬間算完。為什麼？
[x] 直接遞迴會重算同樣的 $f(k)$ 非常多次（呼叫次數指數級）；存起來後每個 $f(k)$ 只算一次，共 50 次
[ ] 因為存起來的版本用了更快的電腦
[ ] 因為 50 太小
[ ] 兩者其實一樣快
---
直接遞迴的呼叫次數大約跟答案本身同量級（約 $2\times10^{10}$）。記憶化把指數時間變成線性時間，是 DP 最基本的效果。


@q dp2-36 | al-dp#2 | 2 | 情境
拼字檢查器用編輯距離（插入、刪除、替換各算 1）找最接近的字。打錯的 `teh` 和 `the` 的編輯距離是多少？
== 2
---
`teh` → `the`：把 e 換成 h、h 換成 e，兩次替換。一般的編輯距離沒有「交換相鄰兩字」的操作；有些拼字檢查器另外加上這種操作，這時距離是 1。


@q dp3-32 | al-dp#3 | 3 | 情境
Two DNA sequences, each with $10^6$ bases, are to be aligned optimally with an LCS-like dynamic program, and the alignment itself, not only its score, is required. The full table has $10^{12}$ entries, far too many to keep in the 128 GB of memory available. The team considers keeping only two rows of the table, using Hirschberg's method, which finds where an optimal path crosses the middle row and then recurses on the two halves, or choosing the best-looking local match at every step, and it wants to compare their memory, running time, and correctness. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Keeping only two rows of the table gives the optimal score with $O(n)$ memory.
[x] Hirschberg's method recovers the alignment with $O(n)$ memory and $O(n^2)$ time.
[x] Hirschberg's method takes roughly twice the time of filling the table once.
[ ] Choosing the best-looking local match at every step gives an optimal alignment.
[ ] With only two rows kept, the alignment can still be recovered by the usual backtracking from the last entry.
---
「兩列算分數，$O(n)$ 記憶體」對：每一格只依賴上一列和左邊。

「Hirschberg 找中間列的交叉點再分治」對：從左上往下算到中間列、從右下往上算到中間列，兩邊相加最好的那一格就是最佳路徑經過的位置；切成兩個子問題遞迴，記憶體始終 $O(n)$。

「時間約兩倍」對：第一層算整張表一次，之後兩個子問題合起來是一半，再下一層是四分之一……總和約兩倍，仍是 $O(n^2)$。

「每步挑當下最好的配對」錯：貪心不保證全域最佳，這正是要用 DP 的原因。

「兩列也能倒推」錯：倒推需要走回去的那些格子，已經被丟掉了。


@q gd2-28 | al-greedy#2 | 1 | 情境

[x] Repeatedly picking the compatible meeting that ends earliest is guaranteed to be optimal.
[x] Ending early frees the room as soon as possible, leaving the most chances for later meetings.
[ ] Repeatedly picking the shortest meeting is guaranteed to be optimal.
[ ] Repeatedly picking the meeting that starts earliest is guaranteed to be optimal.
[ ] Picking meetings at random is guaranteed to be optimal.
---
早結束讓會議室最早空出來，留給後面最多的機會，有交換論證保證最佳，「Repeatedly picking the compatible…」、「Ending early frees the…」對。最短的會議可能卡在兩場長會議中間；最早開始的可能佔滿整天；隨便挑當然不保證，「Repeatedly picking the shortest…」、「Repeatedly picking the meeting…」、「Picking meetings at random…」錯。


@q gd4-27 | al-greedy#4 | 2 | 情境

[x] This is Kruskal's algorithm run in decreasing order of weight.

[ ] The greedy rule may fail, so dynamic programming is required.

@q sp1-27 | al-sp#1 | 2 | 情境

For example, if the best known times are 7 minutes to $u$ and 15 minutes to $v$, and the road from $u$ to $v$ takes 5 minutes, the app changes the time for $v$ to 12. The developers want to name this step correctly and to understand how the different shortest-path algorithms use it. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] This update is called relaxation.
[x] Relaxing edge $(u,v)$: if $d[u]+w(u,v)<d[v]$, set $d[v]=d[u]+w(u,v)$.
[ ] This update is called backtracking.
[x] Shortest-path algorithms differ mainly in which edges they relax and in what order.
[ ] Relaxation can increase $d[v]$.
---
「叫鬆弛」對。

「定義」對。

「回溯」錯：那是搜尋時退回上一步。

「演算法差在順序」對：Dijkstra、Bellman–Ford、DAG 都是在決定「用什麼順序鬆弛哪些邊」。

@q sp4-30 | al-sp#4 | 3 | 情境
A sparse graph has $V=10^4$ vertices and $E=5\times10^4$ edges, with some negative edge weights but no negative-weight cycle, and we need the shortest distance between every pair of vertices. Take $\lg V\approx13$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] A method with three nested loops over the vertices takes about $V^3=10^{12}$ steps.
[x] Johnson's algorithm takes about $VE\lg V\approx7\times10^9$ steps.
[ ] The two take about the same number of steps.
[x] Johnson's algorithm runs Bellman–Ford once, about $VE=5\times10^8$ steps, and then Dijkstra's algorithm from every vertex.
[ ] Johnson's algorithm cannot be used, because some weights are negative.
---
「三層迴圈約 $10^{12}$」對：這就是 Floyd–Warshall。

「Johnson 約 $7\times10^9$」對：每次 Dijkstra 約 $E\lg V$，跑 $V$ 次。

「差不多」錯：差一百多倍。稀疏圖時 $VE\lg V\ll V^3$；很稠密的圖則兩者差不多，Floyd–Warshall 程式更簡單。

@q dp1-34 | al-dp#1 | 3 | 比較
Consider the 0/1 knapsack recurrence $K(i, w) = \max\bigl(K(i-1, w),\ v_i + K(i-1, w - w_i)\bigr)$, where the second term is used only when $w_i \le w$, and $K(0, w) = 0$. There are $n = 4$ items with weights $w_1 = 1000$, $w_2 = 2000$, $w_3 = 3000$, $w_4 = 4000$, and the weight limit is $W = 10^6$. Algorithm P fills the entire table $K(i, w)$ for $0 \le i \le 4$ and $0 \le w \le W$ bottom-up. Algorithm Q is the top-down recursion with memoization, started from $K(4, W)$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Algorithm P computes $5(10^6 + 1)$ table entries.
[x] Algorithm Q computes only 26 distinct subproblems $K(i, w)$, including the base cases with $i = 0$.
[ ] Algorithm Q computes exactly $1 + 2 + 4 + 8 + 16 = 31$ distinct subproblems, because each call makes at most two recursive calls.
[ ] Memoization cannot be used here, since it only works when the subproblems do not overlap.
[x] If almost every table entry were needed for the final answer, algorithm P would usually run faster than Q in practice, because it has no function-call overhead.
---
「P 算 $5(10^6+1)$ 格」對：$i$ 有 0～4 共 5 列，$w$ 有 0～$10^6$ 共 $10^6+1$ 行，由下往上不管用不用得到，整張表都填。

「Q 只算 26 個」對、「剛好 31 個」錯：從 $K(4,W)$ 往下，第 $i$ 層出現的容量是 $W$ 減掉「第 $i+1$～4 個物品某個子集合的重量和」。第 4 層 1 個；第 3 層 $\{0,4000\}$ 共 2 個；第 2 層 4 個；第 1 層 $\{2000,3000,4000\}$ 的子集合和 0、2、3、4、5、6、7、9（千）共 8 個；第 0 層 $\{1,2,3,4\}$（千）的子集合和是 0～10 共 11 個，例如 $1+4=2+3$ 撞在一起。$1+2+4+8+11=26$。用「每次最多分兩支」去算 31，是沒扣掉重疊的子問題——這正是記憶化省下來的部分。

「記憶化不能用在重疊子問題」錯：剛好相反，子問題重疊才需要記下答案；不重疊的話根本不會重算。

「幾乎每格都要用時 P 比較快」對：兩邊算的格數差不多時，由下往上只是兩層迴圈，省掉遞迴呼叫和查表是否算過的成本，也比較容易只留兩列來省空間。



@q gd2-29 | al-greedy#2 | 3 | 比較

[x] Mapping each activity $[s,f)$ to $[-f,-s)$ turns "starts latest" into "finishes earliest".
[x] Always choosing the activity that starts earliest is not always optimal.
[ ] Choosing the latest start always gives fewer activities than choosing the earliest finish.
[ ] Choosing the latest start may select overlapping activities.
---
把時間軸倒過來，問題本質不變，「最晚開始」就是「最早結束」，交換論證一樣適用，「Working from the back…」、「Mapping each activity $[s,f)$…」對、「always fewer」錯；它只選和已選的不衝突的活動，不會重疊。「最早開始」有反例：$[0,10)$、$[1,2)$、$[3,4)$，先選 $[0,10)$ 只得 1 個，最佳 2 個，「Always choosing the activity…」對。

@q rc2-36 | al-recurrence#2 | 2 | 比較
$T_1(n)=2T_1(n/2)+1$、$T_2(n)=2T_2(n/2)+n$、$T_3(n)=2T_3(n/2)+n^2$。三者的解依序是？
[x] $\Theta(n)$、$\Theta(n\lg n)$、$\Theta(n^2)$
[ ] 都是 $\Theta(n\lg n)$
[ ] $\Theta(\lg n)$、$\Theta(n)$、$\Theta(n^2)$
[ ] $\Theta(n)$、$\Theta(n)$、$\Theta(n^2\lg n)$
---

@q rc4-31 | al-recurrence#4 | 3 | 比較
Let $T_1(n)=T_1(n/2)+1$ and $T_2(n)=T_2(\sqrt n)+1$ above size two, with both base values one. Evaluate on $n=2^{64}$, stopping exactly at two. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The first recurrence makes 63 nonbase reductions.
[x] The second recurrence makes six nonbase reductions.
[x] The two asymptotic costs are $\Theta(\lg n)$ and $\Theta(\lg\lg n)$, respectively.
[ ] Both recurrences have total value 64 at this input.
[ ] Taking a square root subtracts one from the base-two logarithm of the current size.

「第一六十三次」對：指數 64 每次減一，降到一共六十三次；總值含基底為六十四。「第二六次」對：指數 64→32→16→8→4→2→1，總值七。「對數與雙對數」對：一個對半原規模，一個對半其對數。「都六十四」錯：第二個只有七。「平方根使對數減一」錯：會除二，原規模除二才使對數減一。


@q sp1-28 | al-sp#1 | 2 | 比較
Consider three ways to order relaxations for single-source shortest paths. Method 1 relaxes every edge, repeated for $V-1$ rounds. Method 2 processes the vertices in an order in which every edge goes from an earlier vertex to a later one, relaxing each vertex's outgoing edges once. Method 3 repeatedly picks the unprocessed vertex with the smallest distance estimate and relaxes its outgoing edges. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Method 1 gives correct distances whenever no negative-weight cycle is reachable from the source.
[x] Method 2 requires the graph to have no directed cycle, but it allows negative edge weights.
[x] Method 3 requires every edge weight to be nonnegative.
[ ] Method 3 gives correct distances even with negative edge weights, as long as there is no negative-weight cycle.
[ ] Method 2 takes $O(VE)$ time.
---
三種分別是 Bellman–Ford、DAG 最短路徑、Dijkstra。順序越聰明，需要的鬆弛次數越少，但前提越多。

「方法一：沒有負環就行」對：最通用，但最慢，$O(VE)$。

「方法二：不能有有向環，可以有負邊」對：照拓樸順序，每個點處理時它的前驅都已經定案。

「方法三：不能有負邊」對：它靠的是「取出時距離已經不會再變小」。

「方法三沒有負環就能有負邊」錯：例如邊 $s\to a$ 權重 1、$s\to b$ 權重 3、$b\to a$ 權重 $-3$、$a\to c$ 權重 1。先取出 $a$（距離 1）並用它把 $c$ 設成 2、取出 $c$；最後取出 $b$ 才發現 $a$ 其實是 0，但 $a$ 不會再處理一次，$c$ 停在 2，正確是 1。

「方法二 $O(VE)$」錯：每條邊只鬆弛一次，加上拓樸排序，$O(V+E)$。



@q gd6-27 | al-greedy#6 | 2 | 反例
The only coin values are 3 and 5, and the amount 9 must be made. The greedy rule repeatedly takes the largest coin not exceeding the remaining amount. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Greedy takes 5, then 3, and is stuck with 1 left.
[x] The amount 9 can be made as 3+3+3.
[x] Dynamic programming, which tries both coins at every amount, finds a way to make 9.
[ ] Greedy takes 5+3+1.
[ ] The amount 9 cannot be made with these coins.
---
先拿 5，剩 4 只能拿 3，剩 1 就卡住，「Greedy takes 5, then…」對；沒有 1 元硬幣，「5+3+1」錯。3+3+3 就湊得出來，DP 會檢查「拿 3」「拿 5」兩種選擇，發現可行，「3+3+3」「dynamic programming」對、「cannot be made」錯。貪婪一步走錯就回不了頭。


@q gd6-28 | al-greedy#6 | 3 | 計算
Coin denominations are 1, 4, 7. A program compares the greedy coin count with the minimum coin count for every amount from 1 to 300. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Greedy is optimal for every amount from 1 to 300.
[x] For 8, greedy uses 7+1, and the optimum 4+4 also uses two coins.
[x] With denominations 1, 4, 6 instead, greedy already fails at 8.
[ ] With 1, 4, 7, greedy first fails at 8.
[ ] With 1, 4, 7, greedy fails at 12, using 3 coins where 2 suffice.
---
窮舉 1～300 找不到任何反例，「Greedy is optimal for…」對、「With 1, 4, 7, greedy first…」、「With 1, 4, 7, greedy fails…」錯。8 元：7+1 兩枚，4+4 也是兩枚；12 元：7+4+1 三枚，4+4+4 也三枚，最少就是 3 枚。三種面額 $1<a<b$ 時，令 $b=qa+r$（$0\le r<a$），貪婪永遠最佳的條件是 $r=0$ 或 $a-r\le q$：$7=1\cdot4+3$，$4-3=1\le1$ 成立；1、4、6：$6=1\cdot4+2$，$4-2=2>1$，8 元時貪婪 6+1+1 三枚、最佳 4+4 兩枚，「With denominations 1, 4,…」對。



@q np4-28 | al-np#4 | 3 | 計算
全集 $\{1,2,3,4,5,6\}$，集合 $S_1=\{1,2,3\}$、$S_2=\{4,5,6\}$、$S_3=\{1,2,4,5\}$。貪婪做法每次挑「蓋住最多還沒被蓋的元素」的集合，直到全部蓋住。貪婪用了幾個集合？最少需要幾個？
== 3,2
?? 貪婪用的個數,最少個數
---
第一步 $S_3$ 蓋住 4 個最多，被選。剩 $\{3,6\}$：$S_1$、$S_2$ 各只蓋 1 個，只好兩個都選，共 3 個。最佳是 $S_1,S_2$ 兩個。貪婪被「一開始看起來最划算」的 $S_3$ 誤導。


@q np4-29 | al-np#4 | 2 | 追蹤
點覆蓋的 2-近似（每次挑一條還沒被蓋住的邊，把兩端都加入）用在 $K_4$（4 個點兩兩相連）。得到的覆蓋大小、最佳覆蓋大小各是？
== 4,3
?? 近似演算法得到的,最佳
---
挑邊 (1,2)，加入 1、2；這時邊 (3,4) 還沒被蓋到，再挑它，加入 3、4，共 4 個。$K_4$ 任選 3 個點就能蓋住所有邊（剩下的那個點的邊另一端都在覆蓋裡），只選 2 個點的話，另外兩個點之間的那條邊沒人蓋，所以最佳是 3，比值 $\frac43\le2$。


@q np4-30 | al-np#4 | 3 | 情境
0/1 背包容量 10。物品 A 重 1、價值 2；物品 B 重 10、價值 10。「依價值／重量由大到小，放得下就拿」會得到多少？怎麼簡單修正就能保證至少有最佳解的一半？
[x] 只拿到 A（價值 2）；改成「貪婪結果」和「單拿價值最高的一件」取較好的那個
[ ] 拿到 B（價值 10），不需要修正
[ ] 拿到 A 和 B（價值 12）
[ ] 改成依重量由小到大拿
---
A 的比值 2、B 的比值 1，先拿 A，剩容量 9 放不下 B，只得 2，最佳是 10。差距可以任意大（把 B 的重量和價值一起放大）。修正版取 $\max$(貪婪, 最貴的一件)，可以證明至少是最佳解的 $\frac12$。



@q rc2-37 | al-recurrence#2 | 3 | 證明
用遞迴樹說明 $T(n)=4T(n/2)+n$ 的解是 $\Theta(n^2)$。
---
第 $i$ 層有 $4^i$ 個子問題，每個大小 $n/2^i$，這一層的工作是 $4^i\cdot\frac n{2^i}=2^in$：每往下一層變成 2 倍，是遞增的等比級數。共 $\lg n$ 層，總和 $n(1+2+\cdots+2^{\lg n})<n\cdot2\cdot2^{\lg n}=2n^2$，而最底層 $4^{\lg n}=n^2$ 個葉子就已經是 $\Theta(n^2)$。所以由最後一層主導，$T(n)=\Theta(n^2)$，對應主定理第一種情況。


@q dc3-29 | al-dc#3 | 3 | 證明
最近點對的分治：用垂直線把點分成左右兩半，各自遞迴得到最近距離 $\delta_L$、$\delta_R$，令 $\delta=\min(\delta_L,\delta_R)$。說明：若有一對「一點在左、一點在右」的點距離小於 $\delta$，兩點到分隔線的水平距離都小於 $\delta$。
---
設 $p$ 在左、$q$ 在右，分隔線 $x=\ell$，所以 $p_x\le\ell\le q_x$。兩點距離 $\ge|q_x-p_x|=(\ell-p_x)+(q_x-\ell)$。若距離 $<\delta$，則 $\ell-p_x<\delta$、$q_x-\ell<\delta$，兩點都落在寬 $2\delta$ 的帶狀區域裡。所以合併時只要檢查帶狀區域內的點。


@q dp3-33 | al-dp#3 | 2 | 證明
LCS 的表 $L[i][j]$ 填完後，從 $(m,n)$ 往回走重建一個最長共同子序列：若 $x_i=y_j$ 就輸出這個字元並走到 $(i-1,j-1)$；否則走到 $L[i-1][j]$、$L[i][j-1]$ 中較大的那一格。說明這個過程只要 $O(m+n)$ 步，而且輸出的長度正好是 $L[m][n]$。
---
每一步 $i$ 或 $j$ 至少減 1，最多 $m+n$ 步就走到邊界。長度：$x_i=y_j$ 時 $L[i][j]=L[i-1][j-1]+1$，輸出一個字元、值減 1；不相等時 $L[i][j]=\max(L[i-1][j],L[i][j-1])$，走向較大者值不變、不輸出。所以輸出的字元數等於從 $L[m][n]$ 降到 0 的次數，正好 $L[m][n]$ 個，而且依倒序都是兩邊共同出現的字元。


@q sp2-33 | al-sp#2 | 3 | 證明
給一個反例說明 Dijkstra 在有負權重的邊時可能算錯，並指出它的正確性證明中哪一步失效。
---
反例：s→a 權重 2、s→b 權重 3、b→a 權重 −2。Dijkstra 先取出 s，再取出 a（$d=2$）並把它當成確定；之後取出 b（$d=3$），鬆弛 b→a 得到 1，但 a 已經被確定過，最後回報的 2 是錯的（正確是 1）。證明中的關鍵是「取出 $u$ 時，任何還沒取出的點 $y$ 都有 $d[y]\ge d[u]$，從 $y$ 再走下去路徑只會變長」；有負邊時「再走下去只會變長」不成立。


@q sp4-31 | al-sp#4 | 2 | 證明
Johnson 演算法把每條邊改成 $\hat w(u,v)=w(u,v)+h(u)-h(v)$。證明：對任意兩點 $s,t$，每一條 $s$ 到 $t$ 的路徑權重都剛好變化 $h(s)-h(t)$，所以原本的最短路徑仍然是最短路徑。
---
路徑 $s=v_0,v_1,\dots,v_k=t$ 的新權重 $\sum_{i=1}^k\bigl(w(v_{i-1},v_i)+h(v_{i-1})-h(v_i)\bigr)=\sum w(v_{i-1},v_i)+h(v_0)-h(v_k)$，中間的 $h$ 一正一負全部消掉（伸縮和）。每一條 $s\to t$ 路徑都加上同一個常數 $h(s)-h(t)$，大小順序不變；環的權重（$s=t$）則完全不變，所以負環也不會被製造或消失。


@q pb1-33 | al-prob#1 | 3 | 證明
$n$ 個互不相同的數隨機打亂順序（每種順序機率相同），依序看過去。若第 $i$ 個數比前面所有數都大，就叫它一個「新紀錄」。用指示隨機變數證明新紀錄的期望個數是 $H_n=1+\frac12+\cdots+\frac1n$。
---
令 $X_i=1$ 若第 $i$ 個是新紀錄，否則 0。前 $i$ 個數的相對順序也是均勻隨機的，其中最大的那個落在第 $i$ 個位置的機率是 $\frac1i$，所以 $E[X_i]=\frac1i$。新紀錄總數 $X=\sum X_i$，由期望值的線性 $E[X]=\sum_{i=1}^n\frac1i=H_n\approx\ln n$。這就是「雇用問題」中平均要換幾次人。


@q np3-31 | al-np#3 | 2 | 證明
說明判斷一張無向圖能不能用 2 種顏色著色（相鄰的點不同色）可以在多項式時間完成，並說明演算法說「不能」時為什麼一定是對的。
---
對每個連通塊任選一點塗紅色，一層一層往外：鄰居塗相反色。每個點、每條邊只處理常數次，時間 $O(V+E)$。若某條邊兩端被塗成同色，沿著兩端回到起點的兩條路徑長度奇偶相同，加上這條邊形成一個奇數長度的環；奇環上顏色必須交替，繞一圈回來會衝突，所以任何 2 著色都不可能。沒有衝突時，塗出來的就是合法著色。


@q dp2-37 | al-dp#2 | 3 | 證明
矩陣鏈乘 $M_1M_2\cdots M_n$（$M_i$ 是 $p_{i-1}\times p_i$）。說明遞迴式 $m[i][j]=\min_{i\le k<j}\bigl(m[i][k]+m[k+1][j]+p_{i-1}p_kp_j\bigr)$ 的正確性，以及為什麼 $O(n^3)$。
---
任何一種括法，最後一次乘法一定是把某個 $M_i\cdots M_k$ 的結果乘上 $M_{k+1}\cdots M_j$ 的結果，代價 $p_{i-1}p_kp_j$。最佳括法中，左右兩段各自也必須是最佳括法（否則換成更好的括法，總代價更小，矛盾）。所以對所有 $k$ 取最小值即可。狀態有 $O(n^2)$ 個 $(i,j)$，每個要試 $O(n)$ 個 $k$，總共 $O(n^3)$。



@q rc3-30 | al-recurrence#3 | 2 | 反例
有人說：「遞迴樹的總工作量＝層數 × 第一層的工作量。」用 $T(n)=2T(n/2)+n^2$ 檢查，正確的答案是？
[x] $\Theta(n^2)$：每往下一層工作量減半，總和是等比級數，由第一層主導
[ ] $\Theta(n^2\lg n)$：$\lg n$ 層、每層 $n^2$
[ ] $\Theta(n\lg n)$
[ ] $\Theta(n^3)$
---
第 $i$ 層有 $2^i$ 個子問題、每個 $(n/2^i)^2$，該層工作 $n^2/2^i$。各層是 $n^2,\frac{n^2}2,\frac{n^2}4,\dots$，總和 $<2n^2$。只有每層工作量都一樣時（例如 $2T(n/2)+n$）才是「層數 × 每層」。


@q dc1-31 | al-dc#1 | 3 | 反例
Someone says: "Merge sort should split into as many parts as possible: split into $n$ parts of one element each and merge them all at once." In the merge, each output step scans the fronts of all parts to find the minimum. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The total time is $\Theta(n^2)$: $n$ elements are output, each after scanning $n$ parts.
[x] This is essentially repeatedly picking the smallest remaining element.
[x] Splitting into $k$ parts and merging by scanning costs $\Theta(nk)$ per merge.
[ ] The total time is $\Theta(n\lg n)$, the same as splitting into two.
[ ] The total time is $\Theta(n)$, because there is only one merge.
---
輸出 $n$ 個元素，每次掃 $n$ 份，$\Theta(n^2)$，每次挑剩下最小的，其實就是選擇排序，「The total time is $\Theta(n^2)$:…」、「This is essentially repeatedly…」對、$\Theta(n\lg n)$、$\Theta(n)$ 錯。切成 $k$ 份時，用「掃一遍找最小」合併要 $\Theta(nk)$，「Splitting into $k$ parts…」對。分治的好處來自「切成固定幾份、遞迴很多層」，每層合併只要 $O(n)$。


@q dp2-38 | al-dp#2 | 1 | 反例
The edit distance between two strings of lengths $m$ and $n$ is the fewest insertions, deletions, and substitutions, each with cost 1, needed to turn one string into the other. A student wants quick bounds before filling the $(m+1)\times(n+1)$ table, and guesses that the distance always equals $|m-n|$, the difference of the lengths. To check the guess, the student works out a few small cases by hand, including `abc` versus `xyz`, `a` versus `abc`, and `kitten` versus `sitting`, whose distance is 3 although the lengths differ by only 1. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The strings `abc` and `xyz` show that the edit distance can be larger than the difference of the lengths.
[x] The difference of the lengths is never larger than the edit distance.
[ ] The edit distance always equals the difference of the lengths.
[x] The edit distance is at most the length of the longer string.
[ ] The edit distance between `a` and `abc` is 3.
---
「`abc` 和 `xyz` 是反例」對：長度差 0，但三個位置都要替換，距離 3。

「長度差是下界」對：至少要插入或刪除那麼多次。

「一定等於長度差」錯：內容不同的字元還要替換。

「不超過較長字串的長度」對：短的那段逐位替換，再插入（或刪除）剩下的，共 $\max(m,n)$ 次。

「`a` 和 `abc` 是 3」錯：插入 `b`、`c` 兩次就好，距離 2。


@q dp3-34 | al-dp#3 | 3 | 反例
Someone claims: "When computing an LCS with only two rows of the table kept, we can still trace back from the last entry to rebuild the LCS itself." For strings of lengths $m = 3000$ and $n = 2000$, the two-row version stores about 4000 entries instead of about $6\times10^6$. A classmate disagrees, and a third student mentions that linear-space methods such as Hirschberg's can still rebuild an optimal subsequence. They also test the two-row program on the strings `aa` and `a`, whose LCS has length 1, to check the length it reports. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] The final two table rows always preserve every predecessor decision from all discarded rows.
[x] Two rows suffice to compute the LCS length.
[ ] Every linear-space LCS method necessarily loses the ability to reconstruct any optimal subsequence.
[ ] Hirschberg reconstruction requires retaining the entire quadratic-size table simultaneously.
[ ] For strings aa and a, two-row computation gives LCS length two.
---
「兩列可算長度」對：新列只需要前列及本列已算部分。「末兩列保留所有路徑」錯：被覆蓋的列及其方向沒有自動留下。「線性空間都無法重建」錯：Hirschberg 可用分治和重算找出分界。「Hirschberg 留整張表」錯：它正以線性額外空間重建，不同於直接整表回溯。「aa、a 長度二」錯：a 只有一字元，最長共同子序列長度為一。


@q dp5-32 | al-dp#5 | 3 | 反例

Record original indices as well as values, so that a summary such as [1,4] can be checked against input order. A minimum possible tail for one length need not belong to the same subsequence as the minimum tail for another length. Compare length-only output with reconstructing one actual subsequence. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] At the end, tail is [1, 4].
[x] The length of tail, 2, equals the length of a longest increasing subsequence.
[ ] At the end, tail itself is always an increasing subsequence of the input.
[x] To rebuild an actual longest increasing subsequence, the algorithm should also record each element's predecessor.

「長度 2 正確」對：3, 4 就是一個 LIS。

「[3, 4, 1]」錯：tail 永遠是遞增的。


@q gd3-32 | al-greedy#3 | 2 | 反例
Huffman's algorithm is run on the frequencies a: 1, b: 1, c: 2, d: 2. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Ties during merging allow Huffman trees of different shapes.
[x] One Huffman tree gives codeword lengths 2, 2, 2, 2, and another gives 3, 3, 2, 1 for a, b, c, d.
[x] Every Huffman tree for these frequencies has total encoded length 12.
[ ] The Huffman tree for a given set of frequencies is always unique.
[ ] Huffman's algorithm cannot proceed when there is a tie.
---
先合併 a、b 得 2。這時有三個 2（ab、c、d）：合併 ab 和 c 得 4，再和 d 合併，碼長 3、3、2、1，總長 $3+3+4+2=12$；合併 c 和 d 得 4，再和 ab 合併，碼長都是 2，總長 $6\times2=12$，「Ties during merging allow…」、「One Huffman tree gives…」、「Every Huffman tree for…」對。最佳的總長唯一，樹不一定唯一；平手時隨便挑一個都能繼續，「The Huffman tree for…」、「Huffman's algorithm cannot proceed…」錯。


@q mf1-34 | al-mst-flow#1 | 2 | 反例
有人說：「權重最大的那條邊一定不在最小生成樹裡。」什麼情況是反例？
[x] 那條邊拿掉後圖就不連通：例如它是唯一連到某個點的邊，不管多重都一定要選
[ ] 圖裡所有邊權重都不同時
[ ] 圖是一個環的時候
[ ] 永遠不會有反例
---
cycle property 說的是「某個**環**上權重唯一最大的邊不在 MST 裡」。不在任何環上的邊（橋）一定在每棵生成樹裡，不管它多重。


@q mf2-30 | al-mst-flow#2 | 2 | 反例
Consider the network with only the edges $s\to a$ (limit 1) and $a\to t$ (limit 1), which models a single water main that carries 1 unit per second through one pumping station $a$ in the middle. A student lists every $s$-$t$ cut, that is, every set $S$ that contains $s$ but not $t$, and computes its weight, the total limit of the edges going from $S$ to the rest. The student then asks whether minimum cuts and maximum flows are unique in general, and tries a second network in which two parallel paths of limit 1 join $s$ to $t$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The cuts with source sides $\{s\}$ and $\{s,a\}$ both have value 1, so both are minimum cuts.
[ ] Only the cut with source side $\{s\}$ is a minimum cut.
[x] The maximum flow value is 1.
[x] In general, the maximum flow value is unique, but there can be several minimum cuts.
[ ] Every flow network has exactly one maximum flow.
---
「兩個割都是 1」對：$\{s\}$ 切斷 $s\to a$，$\{s,a\}$ 切斷 $a\to t$。

「只有 $\{s\}$」錯：見上。

「最大流 1」對。

「值唯一、最小割可能很多」對：這個網路就是例子。

「最大流只有一個」錯：例如邊 $s\to x$、$x\to a$、$x\to b$、$a\to t$、$b\to t$ 都限 1，最大流是 1，可以走 $a$ 也可以走 $b$。


@q mf3-30 | al-mst-flow#3 | 2 | 反例
A bipartite graph has left vertices 1, 2, 3, right vertices $a$, $b$, $c$, and edges 1–$a$, 2–$a$, 3–$a$, 3–$b$, 3–$c$. Someone claims: "If every vertex of a bipartite graph with $n$ vertices on each side has at least one edge, the graph has a perfect matching." Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Every vertex in this graph has a neighbor, but no perfect matching exists.
[x] The left set $\{1,2\}$ has only neighbor a and violates Hall\'s requirement.
[x] Edges 1-a and 3-b form a matching of size two.
[x] The maximum matching size is two.
[x] No matching can cover both left vertices one and two, since each can use only right vertex a.
---
「每點有鄰仍不完美」對：一與二都被同一個右點限制。「Hall 失敗」對：兩個左點聯合只有一個鄰點，少於兩個。「一連 a、三連 b」對：兩邊端點皆不重複。「最大為二」對：已構造二且無法同時覆蓋一、二，三不可能。「一二不能同覆蓋」對：匹配中的右點 a 最多被用一次，個別非零度數不保證整組供給足夠。


@q np1-33 | al-np#1 | 2 | 反例
有人說：「問題在 P 裡，就代表實際上一定跑得很快。」為什麼不一定？
[x] 多項式時間也可能是 $n^{100}$，或常數大到不實用；P 只說「隨輸入長度多項式成長」
[ ] P 裡的問題一定是 $O(n)$
[ ] P 裡的問題都比 NP 的難
[ ] P 裡的問題只能在超級電腦上跑
---
P 是理論上的分類：成長速度是多項式。$n^{100}$ 在 $n=10$ 時就是 $10^{100}$ 步。反過來，某些 NP-complete 問題在實務的輸入上靠好的啟發式也跑得很快。分類描述的是「規模變大時的極限行為」。


@q np2-31 | al-np#2 | 2 | 反例
有人說：「$A\le_pB$，而且 $B$ 是 NP-hard，所以 $A$ 也是 NP-hard。」哪一個是反例？
[x] $A$＝「輸入的數是不是偶數」，可以輕易歸約到 SAT（是偶數就輸出一個可滿足的式子，否則輸出不可滿足的），但 $A$ 在 P
[ ] $A$＝3-SAT、$B$＝SAT
[ ] $A$＝SAT、$B$＝3-SAT
[ ] 沒有反例，這句話是對的
---
$A\le_pB$ 的意思是「$A$ 不比 $B$ 難」：能解 $B$ 就能解 $A$。要證明 $A$ 很難，方向要反過來：把一個已知很難的問題歸約**到** $A$。把簡單問題歸約到困難問題，什麼也證明不了。


@q pb1-34 | al-prob#1 | 2 | 反例
Someone claims: "$E[XY]=E[X]E[Y]$ holds for any two random variables, just like $E[X+Y]=E[X]+E[Y]$." Roll one fair die and let $X=Y=$ the number shown. Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[x] $E[XY]=\frac{91}{6}$ while $E[X]\,E[Y]=\frac{49}{4}$, so the claim fails.
[ ] Both sides equal $\frac{49}{4}$, so the claim holds in this example.
[ ] Both sides equal $\frac{91}{6}$, so the claim holds in this example.
[ ] Linearity of expectation also fails here, so $E[X+Y]\ne7$.
[ ] $E[XY]=E[X]E[Y]$ fails even when $X$ and $Y$ are independent.
---
$E[X^2]=\frac{1+4+9+16+25+36}6=\frac{91}6$，$(3.5)^2=\frac{49}4$，「不相等，說法錯」。乘法要獨立（或至少不相關）才能拆開，獨立時一定成立；加法的線性對任何變數都成立，這裡 $E[X+Y]=7$。


@q pb3-31 | al-prob#3 | 2 | 反例
Someone claims: "Naive string search (compare character by character from each starting position, moving on at the first mismatch) takes only $O(n)$ time in the worst case, since the first character usually differs." Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[x] The text of $n$ copies of `a` with the pattern of $m-1$ copies of `a` followed by `b` is a counterexample.
[ ] A text and a pattern with no letter in common form a counterexample.
[ ] A pattern consisting of a single character forms a counterexample.
[ ] A pattern that occurs at the very start of the text forms a counterexample.
[ ] No input is a counterexample, because naive search stops at the first mismatch.
---
「全是 a 的文字配上結尾是 b 的模式」，每個起點都要比到最後的 b 才發現不同，約 $(n-m+1)\cdot m$ 次，$\Theta(nm)$。沒有共同字母、模式只有一個字、模式在開頭，每個起點都只比一兩次，都是 $O(n)$。停在第一個不同的字元並不能保證第一個字就不同。


@q sp5-100 | al-sp#5 | 3 | 反例
有人把 Floyd–Warshall 的三層迴圈寫成 `for i: for j: for k: d[i][j] = min(d[i][j], d[i][k] + d[k][j])`（$k$ 放最內層）。4 個點，邊 1→2、2→4、4→3 權重都是 1。這樣算出的 $d[1][3]$ 是？
[x] $\infty$（正確是 3）
[ ] 3，順序不影響
[ ] 2
[ ] 1
---
算 $d[1][3]$ 時（$i=1,j=3$），要用到 $d[1][4]+d[4][3]$，可是 $d[1][4]$ 要到 $j=4$ 才會被算出來，這時還是 $\infty$；用 $k=2$ 也只有 $d[1][2]+d[2][3]=1+\infty$。結果停在 $\infty$。$k$ 必須在最外層：做完第 $k$ 輪，所有 $d[i][j]$ 才都是「只經過 $1..k$」的最短距離。


@q sp6-29 | al-sp#6 | 3 | 反例
Someone claims: "In a DAG, a path of maximum total weight always starts at a vertex with no incoming edges." Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] With edges $a\to b$ of weight $-5$ and $b\to c$ of weight 3, the maximum-weight path is $b\to c$, and $b$ has an incoming edge.
[x] If every edge weight is nonnegative, some maximum-weight path starts at a vertex with no incoming edges.
[ ] The claim also holds when some weights are negative.
[x] To find the maximum over all paths, every vertex should be allowed as a start, for example by giving every vertex initial value 0.
[ ] With negative weights, a maximum-weight path in a DAG is not defined.
---
「$b\to c$ 是反例」對：接上前面那段反而變短。

「非負權重時可以從源點開始」對：往前多接一段只會更長。

「有負權重也成立」錯：見反例。

「每個點都當起點」對：初值都設 0 再照拓樸順序做。

「沒有定義」錯：DAG 沒有環，路徑有限，最大值一定存在。



@q dp2-39 | al-dp#2 | 2 | 比較
$X=$ `abcd`、$Y=$ `acbd`。「最長共同子序列」（可以跳著取）和「最長共同子字串」（必須連續）的長度各是？
== 3,1
?? 子序列,子字串
---
子序列：`abd`（或 `acd`）長 3。子字串：$X$ 的長度 2 的片段是 ab、bc、cd，$Y$ 的是 ac、cb、bd，沒有共同的，所以只有單一字元，長度 1。子字串的 DP 在字元不同時要歸零（$L[i][j]=0$），子序列則取左、上的最大值。


@q gd2-30 | al-greedy#2 | 2 | 比較
Which of the following statements about selecting non-conflicting activities are correct?
[x] To select the maximum number of non-conflicting activities, the rule "earliest finish time first" is optimal.
[x] When activities have weights, earliest finish first can fail: $[0,2)$ with weight 1 blocks $[1,10)$ with weight 100.
[x] The weighted version can be solved by dynamic programming with $f(j)=\max(f(j-1),\,w_j+f(p(j)))$, where $p(j)$ is the last activity ending before activity $j$ starts.
[ ] Both versions are solved optimally by earliest finish first.
[ ] Both versions are solved optimally by choosing the heaviest activity first.
---
不加權時「選最早結束」的貪婪最佳，「To select the maximum…」對。加了權重後，最早結束的活動可能權重很小，卻擋住一個權重很大的活動，「When activities have weights,…」對；要依結束時間排序，每個活動選或不選做 DP，排序後 $O(n\lg n)$，「The weighted version can…」對。


@q pb1-35 | al-prob#1 | 3 | 比較
4 balls are thrown independently and uniformly at random into 4 bins. Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[x] The expected number of empty bins is $4\cdot\frac{81}{256}\approx1.27$.
[ ] The expected number of empty bins is 1, since 4 balls usually miss exactly one bin.
[ ] A fixed bin is empty with probability $(\frac14)^4$.
[ ] The expected number of nonempty bins is 4, since there are as many balls as bins.
[ ] The expected number of nonempty bins is about 3.27.
---
某箱空的機率是每顆球都沒丟進去：$(\frac34)^4=\frac{81}{256}$，不是 $(\frac14)^4$；4 個箱子加起來「期望 1.27 個空箱」。有球的箱子是 4 減空箱，約 2.73。



@q dc2-32 | al-dc#2 | 2 | 反例
Someone says: "Strassen's $O(n^{2.81})$ beats the ordinary $O(n^3)$, so Strassen should always be used for matrix multiplication." Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Strassen has larger constant factors and needs extra memory, so it can be slower when $n$ is not large.
[x] Practical code switches to ordinary multiplication once the submatrices are below some size.
[x] Its extra additions and subtractions make rounding errors larger when real numbers are used.
[ ] Strassen produces a different product than ordinary multiplication.
[ ] Strassen works only for $2\times2$ matrices.
---
Strassen 用 7 次乘法換掉 8 次，但多了 18 次矩陣加減，常數大又要額外記憶體，實作上子矩陣小於某個大小（幾十到幾百）時改回一般乘法，「Strassen has larger constant…」、「Practical code switches to…」對。數值上誤差界比一般乘法差，對精度敏感的計算要小心，「Its extra additions and…」對。在精確運算下結果一樣；它遞迴地套用到任何 $2^k$ 大小（其他大小補 0）。


@q dc4-32 | al-dc#4 | 1 | 反例
Someone says: "An array of $n$ numbers has at most $n$ inversions." Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] $[4,3,2,1]$ has 6 inversions, more than 4.
[x] A completely reversed array of $n$ numbers has $n(n-1)/2$ inversions.
[x] Hence listing the inversions one by one can take $\Theta(n^2)$ time, while merge sort can count them in $O(n\lg n)$ time.
[ ] $[4,3,2,1]$ has exactly 4 inversions.
[ ] $[4,3,2,1]$ has no inversions.
---
完全倒序時任兩個都是逆序，$[4,3,2,1]$ 有 $\frac{4\times3}2=6$ 個，一般 $\frac{n(n-1)}2$，「$[4,3,2,1]$ has 6 inversions,…」、「A completely reversed array…」對、「$[4,3,2,1]$ has exactly 4…」、「$[4,3,2,1]$ has no inversions.」錯。逆序對可以多到 $\Theta(n^2)$ 個，一個一個列就要這麼久；只要「數」的話，合併排序時順便數，$O(n\lg n)$，「Hence listing the inversions…」對。



@q dp2-40 | al-dp#2 | 3 | 找錯
For the 0/1 knapsack problem (each item can be taken at most once), a student writes $dp[i][w] = \max\bigl(dp[i-1][w],\ dp[i][w - w_i] + v_i\bigr)$ when $w_i \le w$. Consider two items with $(w_1, v_1) = (2, 3)$ and $(w_2, v_2) = (3, 4)$ and weight limit $W = 4$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The student's recurrence lets item $i$ be taken more than once, so it solves the unbounded knapsack problem instead.
[x] The correct second term is $dp[i-1][w - w_i] + v_i$.
[x] For the given instance, the correct optimal value is 4, while the student's recurrence returns 6.
[ ] The student's recurrence still gives the correct answer whenever all item weights are distinct.
[ ] When the 0/1 knapsack table is compressed into a single one-dimensional array, the weight limits $w$ must be processed in increasing order.
---
「學生的式子讓同一件可以拿很多次」對：拿了第 $i$ 件之後還查同一列 $dp[i][\cdot]$，表示剩下的容量還能再用第 $i$ 件，這是無限背包的遞迴式。

「第二項應該是 $dp[i-1][w-w_i]+v_i$」對：拿了第 $i$ 件，剩下的容量只能用前 $i-1$ 件填。

「正確 4、學生 6」對：兩件合起來重 5，超過 4，只能拿一件，最好是第二件的 4；學生的式子把第一件拿兩次，重 4、價值 6。

「重量互異就沒事」錯：上面的例子重量就互異。

「壓成一維時容量由小到大掃」錯：由小到大掃時，$d[w-w_i]$ 已經是這一輪更新過的值，又會變成重複拿；0/1 背包要由大到小掃。（由小到大剛好是無限背包的寫法。）


@q dp5-33 | al-dp#5 | 2 | 找錯
A one-based LIS DP initializes every L[i] to 1, but its update uses every earlier j without checking the input values. Trace this faulty rule on 4,3,2,1 and compare with strict increasing subsequences. Then trace the corrected rule on 1,2,3,0, where a longest subsequence does not end at the final position. An empty set of eligible predecessors contributes zero before adding the current entry. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The condition $a_j<a_i$ is missing: only a smaller $a_j$ can come right before $a_i$.
[x] Without that condition, $L[i]$ simply becomes $i$.
[x] The answer is the maximum of all $L[i]$, not necessarily $L[n]$.
[ ] The maximum should be over $j>i$.
[ ] The 1 should not be added.
---
「漏了 $a_j<a_i$」對：正確是 $L[i]=1+\max\{L[j]:j<i,\ a_j<a_i\}$，沒有這樣的 $j$ 時 $L[i]=1$。

「變成 $i$」對：每個都接在前一個後面，只是在數位置。

「答案是所有 $L[i]$ 的最大值」對：最長的不一定以最後一個結尾。

「$j>i$」錯：是接在前面的。

「不加 1」錯：要算上 $a_i$ 自己。


@q gd6-29 | al-greedy#6 | 2 | 找錯
To find the fewest coins for amount $a$, a student writes $dp[0]=1$ and $dp[a]=1+\min_{c\le a}dp[a-c]$, where $c$ ranges over the denominations. The denominations are 1 and 5. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The code computes $dp[5]=2$.
[x] The correct answer for amount 5 is 1.
[x] The fix is to set $dp[0]=0$.
[x] With $dp[0]=1$, every computed $dp[a]$ is exactly one more than the true minimum.
[ ] The recurrence also needs the base cases $dp[1]=1$ and $dp[5]=1$ to be set by hand.
---
「算出 $dp[5]=2$」對：$dp[1]=1+dp[0]=2$、…、$dp[4]=5$，$dp[5]=1+\min(dp[4],dp[0])=1+1=2$。

「正確答案是 1」對：一枚 5 元就好。

「改成 $dp[0]=0$」對：初值的意思是「金額 0 最少要幾枚」，答案是 0 枚。

「每個值都多 1」對：初值多 1，每個值都是從它一路加上來的，全部跟著多 1。

「還要手動設 $dp[1]$、$dp[5]$」錯：只要 $dp[0]=0$，遞迴式自己就會算出 $dp[1]=1$、$dp[5]=1$。

A student's Floyd–Warshall initializes every $d[i][j]$, including $d[i][i]$, to $\infty$ and then fills in the edge weights. The graph has no negative-weight cycle. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] $d[i][i]$ should start at 0, since staying put reaches $i$.
[x] With this initialization, $d[i][i]$ ends up as the length of the shortest cycle through $i$, or stays $\infty$ if there is none.
[ ] All entries end up as 0.
[x] The entries $d[i][j]$ with $i\ne j$ end with the same values as in the correct version.
[ ] The loops run forever.
---

@q rc2-38 | al-recurrence#2 | 3 | 情境

@q rc4-32 | al-recurrence#4 | 3 | 情境
某種資料結構每次把大小 $u$ 的問題變成大小 $\sqrt u$ 的子問題（只遞迴一次），其他工作 $O(1)$。$u=2^{64}$ 時大約要幾層？
[x] $T(u)=T(\sqrt u)+O(1)=\Theta(\lg\lg u)$，約 6 層（$64\to32\to16\to8\to4\to2\to1$ 位）

[ ] 約 32 層

@q gd5-29 | al-greedy#5 | 2 | 比較

The greedy method takes elements in decreasing order of weight and adds each one if independence is kept. Which of the following statements are correct?

[x] On the graphic matroid, greedy gives a maximum-weight spanning tree (or forest).

[ ] Greedy is not guaranteed optimal on either.
---

@q mf2-31 | al-mst-flow#2 | 2 | 比較
Start a flow network at zero flow, with positive integer limits: s-to-u is 3, s-to-v is 2, u-to-v is 1, u-to-t is 2, and v-to-t is 3. Compare arbitrary residual augmenting paths with paths chosen by fewest edges. Each augmentation uses its positive residual bottleneck and updates reverse residual edges. The comparison concerns final flow value and iteration bounds, rather than requiring identical edge flows or identical path choices. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Both versions end with a flow of the same value, the maximum flow value.
[ ] The fewest-edges version ends with a larger flow.
[x] When the residual network has no augmenting path, the current flow is a maximum flow.
[x] With integer edge values, the arbitrary-path version needs at most $|f^*|$ augmentations, where $|f^*|$ is the maximum flow value.
[ ] With integer edge values, the arbitrary-path version may stop with a flow that is not maximum.
---
「兩者的值一樣」對：差在擴增的次數和時間。

「最短路版本比較大」錯：最大流的值只有一個。

「找不到擴增路徑就是最大流」對：最大流最小割定理；跟怎麼找路無關。

「任意找最多 $|f^*|$ 次」對：整數時每次至少多 1。這個上界和數字大小有關；BFS 版的上界只和 $V$、$E$ 有關。

「可能停在非最大流」錯：整數時一定會停，停下來就是最大流。（只有容量是無理數時，任意找才可能不停或收斂到錯的值。）


@q mf3-31 | al-mst-flow#3 | 3 | 比較
Compare König's theorem and Hall's theorem about bipartite graphs with left side $L$ and right side $R$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] König's theorem: in a bipartite graph, the size of a maximum matching equals the minimum number of vertices needed to touch every edge.
[x] Hall's theorem: there is a matching that covers every vertex of $L$ if and only if $|N(S)| \ge |S|$ for every subset $S \subseteq L$.
[ ] König's theorem holds for every graph, not only bipartite ones.
[x] In a triangle, a maximum matching has 1 edge, but 2 vertices are needed to touch every edge.
[ ] Hall's theorem says that a matching covering $L$ exists if and only if every vertex of $L$ has at least one edge.
---
「König：最大匹配＝最少點覆蓋」對：用最大流最小割可以證明。

「Hall：每一群左點的鄰居都夠多」對。

「König 對所有圖成立」錯：只對二分圖；三角形就是反例。

「三角形 1 對 2」對：三個點兩兩相連，任兩條邊共用一個點，最大匹配只有 1；一個點最多碰兩條邊，要碰到三條至少要 2 個點。

「每點有邊就夠」錯：要每一**群**都夠，例如 1、2 兩點都只連到 $a$ 就不行。


@q pb2-30 | al-prob#2 | 3 | 比較
Two Monte Carlo algorithms answer yes/no questions. Algorithm P always answers "no" when the true answer is "no", and answers "no" with probability $\frac12$ when the true answer is "yes". Algorithm Q may err on either answer, each time with probability $\frac13$. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Whenever P answers "yes", that answer is certainly correct.
[x] Running P $k$ times and answering "yes" if any run says "yes" gives error probability at most $(\frac12)^k$.
[x] For Q, running it many times and taking the majority answer makes the error probability decrease exponentially.
[ ] For P, taking the majority of $k$ runs is the best way to reduce its error.
[ ] For Q, answering "yes" if any run says "yes" also makes the error decrease exponentially.
---
P 在真正是「否」時不會答「是」，所以「答『是』一定對」；只有 $k$ 次全誤答「否」才錯，「$(\frac12)^k$」。對 P 取多數決反而不好，錯的機率約一半。Q 兩邊都會錯，只能「多數決」；用「有一次說是就答是」，真正是「否」時錯的機率反而越跑越高。


@q pb3-32 | al-prob#3 | 3 | 比較
要在一篇長文中同時找 100 個**長度相同**的關鍵字。Rabin–Karp 和 KMP 哪個比較適合？
[x] Rabin–Karp：算一次文字中每個窗格的指紋，拿去和 100 個關鍵字的指紋表比對；KMP 要對每個關鍵字各掃一次
[ ] KMP，因為它最壞情況比較好
[ ] 兩者一樣
[ ] 兩者都不能找多個關鍵字
---
Rabin–Karp 的滾動指紋讓每個窗格 $O(1)$ 算出，再到存了 100 個指紋的表裡查，總時間約 $O(n+100m)$（指紋相同才逐字確認）。KMP 一次只能處理一個模式，100 個就要 100 次 $O(n+m)$。（多個模式還有專門的 Aho–Corasick 演算法。）


@q rc3-31 | al-recurrence#3 | 2 | 比較
$T_1(n)=T_1(n/2)+T_1(n/2)+n$ 和 $T_2(n)=T_2(n/4)+T_2(3n/4)+n$ 的成長等級？
[x] 兩者都是 $\Theta(n\lg n)$
[ ] $T_1$ 是 $\Theta(n\lg n)$、$T_2$ 是 $\Theta(n)$
[ ] $T_1$ 是 $\Theta(n)$、$T_2$ 是 $\Theta(n\lg n)$
[ ] 兩者都是 $\Theta(n^2)$
---
兩個的子問題大小加起來都是 $n$，所以遞迴樹每一層的工作量都是 $n$（$T_2$ 在比較淺的分支結束後，下面的層會略少於 $n$）。$T_2$ 最深的分支深 $\log_{4/3}n$、最淺的 $\log_4n$，都是 $\Theta(\lg n)$，所以總和仍是 $\Theta(n\lg n)$。只有比例和小於 1 時才會降到 $\Theta(n)$。


@q sp6-30 | al-sp#6 | 2 | 比較
In a DAG whose edge weights are all nonnegative, single-source shortest paths are computed by relaxing edges in topological order, and also by Dijkstra's algorithm with a binary heap. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Both methods give the same distances.
[x] The topological-order method takes $O(V+E)$ time, while Dijkstra's algorithm takes $O((V+E)\lg V)$.
[ ] Dijkstra's algorithm gives wrong answers on a DAG.
[x] The topological-order method would still work if some weights were negative.
[ ] The topological-order method fails when the weights are nonnegative.
---
「結果一樣」對：權重非負時兩種方法都正確。

「DAG 方法 $O(V+E)$ 比較快」對：不需要堆積；每個點被處理時所有指向它的邊都已鬆弛過。

「Dijkstra 在 DAG 上錯」錯。

「DAG 方法能處理負權重」對：Dijkstra 不行。

「非負權重時 DAG 方法錯」錯。



@q dp4-29 | al-dp#4 | 2 | 證明
「從 $n$ 個正整數 $w_1,\dots,w_n$ 中挑一些（每個最多一次）湊出 $T$」：令 $dp[i][t]$ 表示「只用前 $i$ 個數能不能湊出 $t$」。說明「$dp[i][t]$ 為真 ⇔ $dp[i-1][t]$ 為真，或 $dp[i-1][t-w_i]$ 為真（$t\ge w_i$ 時）」為什麼正確，以及總時間。
---
用前 $i$ 個數湊出 $t$ 的方法，要嘛沒用到 $w_i$（那就是前 $i-1$ 個湊出 $t$），要嘛用到 $w_i$（剩下的 $t-w_i$ 由前 $i-1$ 個湊）。兩種情況涵蓋全部，所以取「或」。初值 $dp[0][0]$ 為真、$dp[0][t>0]$ 為假。表格 $(n+1)\times(T+1)$ 格，每格 $O(1)$，共 $O(nT)$——對 $T$ 的數值是多項式，對輸入長度（$\lg T$ 位元）卻是指數。


@q gd6-30 | al-greedy#6 | 3 | 證明
證明：面額是 $1,2,4,8,\dots,2^k$ 時，貪婪找零（先拿最大的）一定用最少枚數。
---
看任何一個最佳解。若某種面額 $2^i$（$i<k$）用了 2 枚以上，把 2 枚換成 1 枚 $2^{i+1}$，總額不變、枚數少 1，與最佳矛盾。所以最佳解中除了最大面額外，每種最多 1 枚，也就是金額扣掉 $2^k$ 的倍數後，剩下的部分用的是它的二進位表示，而這正是貪婪的結果（貪婪也是盡量拿 $2^k$，再依二進位由高到低拿）。兩者相同，貪婪最佳。


@q np3-32 | al-np#3 | 3 | 證明
2-SAT：每個子句是兩個文字的「或」，例如 $(a\lor\lnot b)$。把每個子句 $(x\lor y)$ 改寫成兩條「若…則」的箭頭 $\lnot x\to y$、$\lnot y\to x$。說明：如果某個變數 $v$ 能沿著箭頭走到 $\lnot v$，而且 $\lnot v$ 也能走到 $v$，這組子句就不可能同時滿足。
---
每條箭頭 $p\to q$ 都是由子句得到的蘊含：任何滿足所有子句的指派都滿足它。蘊含可以一路串起來，所以「$v$ 走得到 $\lnot v$」表示任何滿足指派中，$v$ 為真就推出 $\lnot v$ 為真，也就是 $v$ 必須為假；同理「$\lnot v$ 走得到 $v$」表示 $v$ 必須為真。兩者矛盾，無解。反方向（沒有這種變數就一定有解）也成立，而檢查可以在多項式時間完成，所以 2-SAT 在 P。


@q pb2-31 | al-prob#2 | 3 | 證明
某個 Las Vegas 演算法答案一定對，執行時間 $T$ 的期望值是 $\mu$。改成「跑到 $2\mu$ 步還沒結束就停下來回答『不知道』」。用不等式「非負隨機變數 $X$ 滿足 $P(X\ge a)\le\frac{\mu_X}a$（$\mu_X$ 是 $X$ 的期望值）」說明它失敗的機率最多 $\frac12$。
---
執行時間 $T\ge0$，Markov 不等式給 $P(T\ge a)\le\frac{\mu}a$。取 $a=2\mu$，$P(T\ge2\mu)\le\frac12$。所以時間上限固定、答錯（說不知道）的機率至多一半：Las Vegas 變成 Monte Carlo。再重複 $k$ 次（每次獨立），全部失敗的機率降到 $2^{-k}$。


@q pb3-33 | al-prob#3 | 2 | 證明
Rabin–Karp 把長度 $m$ 的窗格 $t_s\dots t_{s+m-1}$ 看成以 $d$ 為底的數，指紋 $h_s=\bigl(\sum_{i=0}^{m-1}t_{s+i}d^{m-1-i}\bigr)\bmod q$。說明下一個窗格可以用 $h_{s+1}=\bigl(d(h_s-t_sd^{m-1})+t_{s+m}\bigr)\bmod q$ 在 $O(1)$ 算出來。
---
窗格往右移一格：去掉最高位 $t_s$（它的權重是 $d^{m-1}$），其他每一位權重都乘 $d$（往左移一位），再補上新的最低位 $t_{s+m}$。這三步對整數成立，而取 mod 和加減乘可以交換順序，所以對指紋也成立。$d^{m-1}\bmod q$ 預先算好，每次只做常數次運算，掃完整篇文字 $O(n)$。


@q sp6-31 | al-sp#6 | 2 | 證明
專案排程中，每個工作的「最早開始時間」ES 由前面算到後面取最大值，「最晚開始時間」LS 由後面往前算取最小值。說明關鍵路徑上的工作都滿足 $LS=ES$（寬裕時間為 0），以及為什麼延誤它們一定延誤整個專案。
---
專案總長度＝從起點到終點的最長路徑長度 $L$。在最長路徑上的工作 $j$，它之前那段的最長長度就是 ES，它之後到終點的最長長度是 $L-ES-d_j$，所以它最晚也只能在 $L-(L-ES-d_j)-d_j=ES$ 開始，$LS=ES$。若它晚開始 $\delta$，這條路徑就變長 $\delta$，專案總長至少 $L+\delta$。不在關鍵路徑上的工作有 $LS>ES$，在寬裕時間內延誤不影響總長。



@q dc3-30 | al-dc#3 | 2 | 情境
A flight-monitoring system must find, every second, the two closest of $n = 10^5$ aircraft positions in the plane. One processor core performs about $10^9$ basic operations per second. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Computing the distance of every pair needs about $5\times10^9$ distance computations.
[x] In the divide-and-conquer algorithm, each point in the strip is compared with only a constant number of neighbors in $y$ order.
[x] With $\lg n\approx17$, divide and conquer is roughly 3000 times faster than computing every pair.
[ ] Divide and conquer is slower, because it has to sort.
[ ] The $5\times10^9$ pairwise computations easily fit within one second on one such core.
---
「兩兩比較約 $5\times10^9$」對：$n(n-1)/2$。

「帶狀區域只比常數個」對：分治法依 $x$ 切兩半遞迴，合併時只看寬 $2\delta$ 的帶狀區域，每點只和 $y$ 方向後面常數個點比。

「快約 3000 倍」對：$O(n\lg n)\approx1.7\times10^6$，$5\times10^9/1.7\times10^6\approx2900$。

「要排序所以比較慢」錯：排序只要 $O(n\lg n)$，不會讓它變慢。

「一秒內輕鬆做完」錯：每秒 $10^9$ 次，$5\times10^9$ 次要約 5 秒，趕不上每秒更新。


@q gd1-30 | al-greedy#1 | 2 | 情境
A copy shop has one machine. Three customers arrive together and wait; their jobs take 10, 2 and 5 minutes. We want to minimize the average time until each customer's job is finished. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The jobs should run in the order 2, 5, 10.
[x] The minimum average is $26/3\approx8.7$ minutes.
[x] The last job finishes at minute 17 no matter what the order is.
[ ] The order 10, 5, 2 gives an average of 14 minutes, which is optimal.
[ ] Every order gives the same average.
---
短的排前面，完成時間 2、7、17，平均 $26/3$，「The jobs should run…」、「The minimum average is…」對；交換任何一對「長在前、短在後」的相鄰工作都會讓總和變小（交換論證）。總時間和怎麼排無關（都是 17），「The last job finishes…」對；但平均有關，10、5、2 是 10、15、17，平均 14，最差，「The order 10, 5,…」、「Every order gives the…」錯。


@q pb1-36 | al-prob#1 | 2 | 情境
A company interviews 100 candidates in uniformly random order, all of different ability, and replaces its current hire whenever a better candidate appears. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The $i$-th candidate is hired exactly when they are the best of the first $i$.
[x] The expected number of hires is about 5.19.
[ ] The expected number of hires is about 50, half of the candidates.
[ ] If the candidates arrived in decreasing order of ability, there would be 100 hires.
[ ] The expected number of hires is exactly $\ln 100\approx4.61$.
---
第 $i$ 位會被雇用 ⇔ 「他是前 $i$ 位中最好的」，機率 $\frac1i$；加起來 $H_{100}=1+\frac12+\cdots+\frac1{100}\approx5.19$。$\ln100$ 只是近似，還差約 0.577。能力由好到差排時只雇 1 次；由差到好才是 100 次。


@q rc1-29 | al-recurrence#1 | 2 | 情境
演算法甲剛好做 $n^2$ 步，乙剛好做 $100n\lg n$ 步。$n=1000$ 和 $n=10^4$ 時誰比較快？（$\lg1000\approx10$、$\lg10^4\approx13.3$）
[x] $n=1000$ 時差不多（都約 $10^6$）；$n=10^4$ 時乙快很多（$10^8$ 對 $1.33\times10^7$）
[ ] 甲永遠比較快
[ ] 乙永遠比較快
[ ] $n=10^4$ 時甲比較快
---
$O(n\lg n)$ 比 $O(n^2)$ 好指的是 $n$ 夠大以後。常數 100 讓乙在小規模時吃虧，交叉點大約在 $n\approx1000$；之後差距越拉越大。實務上常在 $n$ 小時改用簡單的方法（例如合併排序在小區段改用插入排序）。



@q gd3-33 | al-greedy#3 | 3 | 情境
一份 10 萬字元的文字檔只用 6 種字元，出現次數（千次）：a 45、b 13、c 12、d 16、e 9、f 5。用固定長度編碼和 Huffman 編碼，各需要多少千位元？
== 300,224
?? 固定長度,Huffman

@q sp2-34 | al-sp#2 | 3 | 追蹤
有向圖的邊（權重都是正的）：a→b 1、a→c 3、a→d 2、b→e 3、c→a 7、d→c 3、s→c 6、s→d 1。從 s 用 Dijkstra 求最短距離。a、b、c、d、e 的距離依序是？
== 11,12,4,1,15
?? a,b,c,d,e
---
每次取出距離最小、還沒確定的點，鬆弛它的出邊。取出順序：s(0) → d(1) → c(4) → a(11) → b(12) → e(15)。權重都非負，所以點一被取出，它的距離就不會再變小。

@q mf1-35 | al-mst-flow#1 | 2 | 追蹤
無向圖 6 個點 a～f，邊和權重：a–d 17、a–e 13、a–f 5、b–c 1、b–d 11、b–e 10、b–f 9、c–e 7、e–f 16。最小生成樹的總權重是多少？
== 33
---

@q dp2-41 | al-dp#2 | 3 | 計算
$X=$ `BCBAAAC`、$Y=$ `CCABADA`。LCS 的長度、編輯距離（插入、刪除、替換各算 1）各是多少？
== 4,4
?? LCS 長度,編輯距離
---
LCS 表 $L[i][j]$：字元相同取左上 +1，否則取左、上較大者；最後一格 4，其中一個 LCS 是 `CBAA`。編輯距離表 $D[i][j]=\min(D[i-1][j]+1,\ D[i][j-1]+1,\ D[i-1][j-1]+[x_i\ne y_j])$，第一列、第一行是 $0,1,2,\dots$；最後一格 4。兩者有關係：只用插入和刪除時，距離是 $7+7-2\times4=6$；允許替換後可能更小。

@q sp2-36 | al-sp#2 | 3 | 追蹤
有向圖的邊（權重都是正的）：a→b 7、a→d 3、a→e 5、b→a 1、b→d 7、c→a 6、c→b 8、c→d 9、d→c 4、d→e 8、s→b 9。從 s 用 Dijkstra 求最短距離。a、b、c、d、e 的距離依序是？
== 10,9,17,13,15
?? a,b,c,d,e
---
每次取出距離最小、還沒確定的點，鬆弛它的出邊。取出順序：s(0) → b(9) → a(10) → d(13) → e(15) → c(17)。權重都非負，所以點一被取出，它的距離就不會再變小。


@q dp2-43 | al-dp#2 | 3 | 計算
$X=$ `CBCCDB`、$Y=$ `BDACBCB`。LCS 的長度、編輯距離（插入、刪除、替換各算 1）各是多少？
== 4,5
?? LCS 長度,編輯距離
---
LCS 表 $L[i][j]$：字元相同取左上 +1，否則取左、上較大者；最後一格 4，其中一個 LCS 是 `CBCB`。編輯距離表 $D[i][j]=\min(D[i-1][j]+1,\ D[i][j-1]+1,\ D[i-1][j-1]+[x_i\ne y_j])$，第一列、第一行是 $0,1,2,\dots$；最後一格 5。兩者有關係：只用插入和刪除時，距離是 $6+7-2\times4=5$；允許替換後可能更小。

@q dc4-33 | al-dc#4 | 2 | 計算
序列 9, 1, 8, 6, 5, 3, 2 有幾個逆序對（$i<j$ 但 $a_i>a_j$）？
== 16
---
對每個數，數它後面有幾個比它小的：9:6、1:0、8:4、6:3、5:2、3:1、2:0，加起來 16。這也正是插入排序（或氣泡排序）要做的交換次數；合併排序可以在 $O(n\lg n)$ 時間內數出來。

== 5

@q dp2-46 | al-dp#2 | 3 | 計算
$M_1$ 是 $6\times15$、$M_2$ 是 $15\times2$、$M_3$ 是 $2\times2$、$M_4$ 是 $2\times3$。算 $M_1M_2M_3M_4$ 最少要做幾次純量乘法？
== 228
---
$m[i][j]=\min_k(m[i][k]+m[k+1][j]+p_{i-1}p_kp_j)$。兩個一組：$m_{12}=180$、$m_{23}=60$、$m_{34}=12$；三個一組：$m_{13}=204$、$m_{24}=102$；全部：228，最佳括法 $((M_1M_2)(M_3M_4))$。

@q rc2-39 | al-recurrence#2 | 3 | 計算
Let $T(n)=7T(n/2)+n$ for powers of two, with $T(1)=1$. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The base-call count is $n^{\log_2 7}$.
[x] $T(4)=67$.
[x] Internal-level work grows by a factor of $7/2$ before the base.
[ ] The tight solution is $\Theta(n\lg n)$ because splitting by two always gives that form.
[ ] The tree has exactly seven base calls for n equal to four.
---
「葉 n 的 log₂7 次方」對：深度 lg n，葉數 7^(lg n)。「六十七」對：T(2)＝7＋2＝9，T(4)＝7×9＋4。「每層七比二」對：子數乘七，單個線性成本除二。「只看除二判 n lg n」錯：還得看分支七與成本，實際 Θ(n^(log₂7))。「四只有七葉」錯：深度二，葉數 7²＝49。


@q rc2-40 | al-recurrence#2 | 2 | 計算
$T(n)=9T(n/4)+n^3\lg n$ 的漸近解是？
[ ] $\Theta(n\lg n)$
[ ] $\Theta(n^2)$
[x] $\Theta(n^3\lg n)$
[ ] $\Theta(n^3\lg n\lg n)$
---
比較 $f(n)=n^3\lg n$ 和 $n^{\log_{4}9}\approx n^{1.58}$：屬於主定理第三種情況（根那一層主導，regularity 條件成立），所以 $T(n)=\Theta(n^3\lg n)$。


@q sp5-102 | al-sp#5 | 3 | 計算
有向圖 4 個點，邊（有負權重、沒有負環）：1→3 7、1→4 1、2→1 7、3→1 4、3→4 8、4→2 -1。用 Floyd–Warshall 求所有點對最短距離。$d[2][3]$ 是多少？
== 14
---
$d^{(k)}[i][j]=\min(d^{(k-1)}[i][j],\ d^{(k-1)}[i][k]+d^{(k-1)}[k][j])$，$k$ 從 1 到 4 依序允許當中間點。最後的距離矩陣第 2 列是 7, 0, 14, 8，所以 $d[2][3]=14$。負權重可以，只要沒有負環（對角線都不小於 0）。


@q sp5-103 | al-sp#5 | 3 | 計算
有向圖 4 個點，邊（有負權重、沒有負環）：1→3 3、2→3 2、2→4 3、3→1 6、3→2 -1、3→4 6、4→2 -1。用 Floyd–Warshall 求所有點對最短距離。$d[2][1]$ 是多少？
== 8
---
$d^{(k)}[i][j]=\min(d^{(k-1)}[i][j],\ d^{(k-1)}[i][k]+d^{(k-1)}[k][j])$，$k$ 從 1 到 4 依序允許當中間點。最後的距離矩陣第 2 列是 8, 0, 2, 3，所以 $d[2][1]=8$。負權重可以，只要沒有負環（對角線都不小於 0）。


@q mf2-32 | al-mst-flow#2 | 3 | 計算
網路的容量：a→b 5、a→c 3、b→a 6、b→c 3、b→t 9、c→t 3、s→b 5、s→c 5。從 s 到 t 的最大流是多少？
== 8
---
用擴增路徑一直推流，直到殘餘網路找不到 s 到 t 的路。最後從 s 在殘餘網路走得到的點是 {c, s}，對應的割切斷 s→b(5)、c→t(3)，容量和 8，正好等於最大流（最大流最小割定理），可以用來驗算。


@q mf2-33 | al-mst-flow#2 | 3 | 計算
網路的容量：b→c 5、b→t 7、c→a 4、c→t 2、s→a 9、s→b 4、s→c 2、s→t 4。從 s 到 t 的最大流是多少？
== 10
---
用擴增路徑一直推流，直到殘餘網路找不到 s 到 t 的路。最後從 s 在殘餘網路走得到的點是 {a, s}，對應的割切斷 s→b(4)、s→c(2)、s→t(4)，容量和 10，正好等於最大流（最大流最小割定理），可以用來驗算。


@q mf3-32 | al-mst-flow#3 | 3 | 計算
4 位工人和 4 項工作，每人只會做某些工作：甲：C；乙：A；丙：A、C；丁：D。每人最多做一項、每項工作最多一人，最多能同時安排幾組？
== 3
---
最大匹配是 3，不到 4。Hall 條件不成立：甲、乙、丙 這 3 個人會做的工作合起來只有 A、C，2 項，不夠分。最大匹配也可以用最大流算：s 連到每位工人、每項工作連到 t，容量都是 1。

For powers of three, $T(n)=9T(n/3)+1$ and $T(1)=1$. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] $T(9)=91$.
[x] The tight solution is $\Theta(n^2)$.
[ ] $T(3)=9$.
[ ] The number of base calls at n equal to nine is nine.
[ ] Constant work per internal call makes the entire recursion $\Theta(\lg n)$ regardless of branching.
---
「九十一」對：T(3)＝9＋1＝10，T(9)＝9×10＋1。「平方」對：基底數 9^(log₃n)＝n²，總樹大小也同階。「T 三等九」錯：漏加根的成本一。「九個基底」錯：兩層各分九支，應有 81 個。「常數單次就全樹對數」錯：呼叫數會分支增加，不能只看單一路徑。


@q rc2-42 | al-recurrence#2 | 2 | 計算
$T(n)=9T(n/2)+n^2$ 的漸近解是？
[x] $\Theta(n^{\log_{2}9})$
[ ] $\Theta(n^3)$
[ ] $\Theta(n^2\lg n)$
[ ] $\Theta(n^2)$
---
比較 $f(n)=n^2$ 和 $n^{\log_{2}9}\approx n^{3.17}$：屬於主定理第一種情況（葉子那一層主導），所以 $T(n)=\Theta(n^{\log_{2}9})$。


@q sp5-104 | al-sp#5 | 3 | 計算
有向圖 4 個點，邊（有負權重、沒有負環）：1→4 8、2→1 -1、4→3 5。用 Floyd–Warshall 求所有點對最短距離。$d[1][3]$ 是多少？
== 13
---
$d^{(k)}[i][j]=\min(d^{(k-1)}[i][j],\ d^{(k-1)}[i][k]+d^{(k-1)}[k][j])$，$k$ 從 1 到 4 依序允許當中間點。最後的距離矩陣第 1 列是 0, ∞, 13, 8，所以 $d[1][3]=13$。負權重可以，只要沒有負環（對角線都不小於 0）。

== 12

@q pb3-34 | al-prob#3 | 2 | 計算
KMP 的失敗函數 $\pi[q]$：模式前 $q$ 個字元中，最長的「既是前綴、又是後綴」（不能是整段）的長度。模式 `AABAAAB` 的 $\pi[1..7]$ 依序是？
== 0 1 0 1 2 2 3
== 0,1,0,1,2,2,3
---
A：0；AA：1（A）；AAB：0；AABA：1（A）；AABAA：2（AA）；AABAAA：前綴 AAB 和後綴 AAA 不同，退回去比較，得 2（AA）；AABAAAB：3（AAB）。算第 6 個時用到「比不上就退到 $\pi[\pi[q]]$ 再比」，這是 KMP 保持線性時間的關鍵。

@q gd3-38 | al-greedy#3 | 2 | 計算
5 種字元的出現次數：a 21、b 17、c 16、d 12、e 7。Huffman 編碼後總共要幾個位元？固定長度編碼要幾個位元？
== 165,219
?? Huffman,固定長度
---
每次合併最小的兩個：依序產生 19、33、40、73。總位元數＝所有合併產生的節點之和＝19+33+40+73＝165（每個字元的次數乘上它的深度，加起來剛好等於這個和）。固定長度：5 種字元要 3 位元，共 73×3＝219。


@q dc4-37 | al-dc#4 | 2 | 計算
序列 7, 4, 1, 9, 2, 5, 8 有幾個逆序對（$i<j$ 但 $a_i>a_j$）？
== 9
---
對每個數，數它後面有幾個比它小的：7:4、4:2、1:0、9:3、2:0、5:0、8:0，加起來 9。這也正是插入排序（或氣泡排序）要做的交換次數；合併排序可以在 $O(n\lg n)$ 時間內數出來。

== 4

@q dp2-50 | al-dp#2 | 3 | 計算
$M_1$ 是 $5\times15$、$M_2$ 是 $15\times4$、$M_3$ 是 $4\times15$、$M_4$ 是 $15\times20$。算 $M_1M_2M_3M_4$ 最少要做幾次純量乘法？
== 1900
---
$m[i][j]=\min_k(m[i][k]+m[k+1][j]+p_{i-1}p_kp_j)$。兩個一組：$m_{12}=300$、$m_{23}=900$、$m_{34}=1200$；三個一組：$m_{13}=600$、$m_{24}=2400$；全部：1900，最佳括法 $((M_1M_2)(M_3M_4))$。

@q dp1-35 | al-dp#1 | 3 | 計算
長度 8 的鋼條，長度 $i$ 的一段可以賣 $p_i$ 元：$p_1=4$、$p_2=11$、$p_3=15$、$p_4=21$、$p_5=22$、$p_6=23$、$p_7=24$、$p_8=25$。怎麼切（也可以不切）賣最多錢？最多是多少？
== 44
---
$r_j=\max_{1\le i\le j}(p_i+r_{j-i})$，$r_0=0$。依序 $r_1=4$、$r_2=11$、$r_3=15$、$r_4=22$、$r_5=26$、$r_6=33$、$r_7=37$、$r_8=44$。最佳切法：2＋2＋2＋2，共 44 元。每個 $r_j$ 只算一次，$O(n^2)$。


@q dp1-36 | al-dp#1 | 3 | 計算
長度 8 的鋼條，長度 $i$ 的一段可以賣 $p_i$ 元：$p_1=1$、$p_2=4$、$p_3=10$、$p_4=12$、$p_5=17$、$p_6=19$、$p_7=21$、$p_8=26$。怎麼切（也可以不切）賣最多錢？最多是多少？
== 27

@q dp4-30 | al-dp#4 | 2 | 計算
從 2, 4, 6, 9 中挑一些（每個最多一次，可以都不挑），可以湊出幾種不同的和（包含 0）？
== 14
---
一個一個加入，維護「目前能湊出的和」：從 $\{0\}$ 開始，每加一個數 $x$，就把現有的每個和加上 $x$ 併進來。最後能湊出 14 種，1 到 21 之間湊不出的有 1, 3, 5, 7, 14, 16, 18, 20。表格法是 $O(nT)$，$T$ 是總和。


@q dp4-31 | al-dp#4 | 2 | 計算
從 4, 6, 10, 11 中挑一些（每個最多一次，可以都不挑），可以湊出幾種不同的和（包含 0）？
== 14
---
一個一個加入，維護「目前能湊出的和」：從 $\{0\}$ 開始，每加一個數 $x$，就把現有的每個和加上 $x$ 併進來。最後能湊出 14 種，1 到 31 之間湊不出的有 1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 8, 9, 12, 13, 18, 19, 22, 23, 24, 26, 28, 29, 30。表格法是 $O(nT)$，$T$ 是總和。

== 5,3

貪婪：7＋1＋1＋1＋1，共 5 枚。用 DP $dp[a]=1+\min_c dp[a-c]$ 得最少 3 枚。面額不是「正規」的組合時，先拿最大的不一定最好。

@q np4-31 | al-np#4 | 3 | 計算
圖的邊依序是 cd、bd、bc、ac、de、ef、ab。點覆蓋的 2-近似：依序看每條邊，若兩端都還沒被選，就把兩端都選進來。得到的覆蓋有幾個點？最小點覆蓋有幾個點？
== 6,3
?? 2-近似,最佳
---

@q dp1-37 | al-dp#1 | 3 | 計算
長度 8 的鋼條，長度 $i$ 的一段可以賣 $p_i$ 元：$p_1=2$、$p_2=6$、$p_3=16$、$p_4=18$、$p_5=20$、$p_6=22$、$p_7=24$、$p_8=27$。怎麼切（也可以不切）賣最多錢？最多是多少？
== 38
---
$r_j=\max_{1\le i\le j}(p_i+r_{j-i})$，$r_0=0$。依序 $r_1=2$、$r_2=6$、$r_3=16$、$r_4=18$、$r_5=22$、$r_6=32$、$r_7=34$、$r_8=38$。最佳切法：3＋3＋2，共 38 元。每個 $r_j$ 只算一次，$O(n^2)$。

@q dc1-36 | al-dc#1 | 2 | 計算
陣列 3, 8, -1, -2, -8, 5, 6, 9, -7。和最大的連續子陣列，和是多少？
== 20
---
最大的一段是第 1 到第 8 個：3, 8, -1, -2, -8, 5, 6, 9，和 20。分治法：答案要嘛在左半、要嘛在右半、要嘛跨中間（從中間往兩邊各找最大的延伸），$O(n\lg n)$；Kadane 掃一遍「以這裡結尾的最大和」可做到 $O(n)$。


@q gd1-35 | al-greedy#1 | 2 | 計算
一台機器要依序處理 5 個工作，處理時間依到達順序是 6, 5, 13, 8, 2（都在時間 0 就到了）。照到達順序做，和照「處理時間短的先做」，各工作完成時間的總和各是多少？
== 107,77
?? 照到達順序,短的先做
---
照到達順序：完成時間 6, 11, 24, 32, 34，總和 107。短的先做（2, 5, 6, 8, 13）：完成時間 2, 7, 13, 21, 34，總和 77，這是最小值：排在第 $i$ 個的工作會被後面 $n-i$ 個工作「等」，所以越短的越該放前面。


@q sp6-36 | al-sp#6 | 3 | 計算
專案有 6 個工作，工期：A 2 天、B 4 天、C 4 天、D 4 天、E 1 天、F 6 天。先後關係（左邊做完右邊才能開始）：A→D、A→E、A→F、B→E、B→F、D→E、E→F。工作可以平行進行，整個專案最少要幾天？
== 13
---
依拓撲順序算每個工作最早開始時間（所有前置工作的「最早開始＋工期」取最大）：A:0、B:0、C:0、D:2、E:6、F:7。專案長度＝最晚結束的那個 ＝13 天。一條關鍵路徑是 A→D→E→F，上面任一工作延誤都會延誤整個專案。



@q mf2-37 | al-mst-flow#2 | 3 | 計算
網路的容量：a→b 9、a→t 3、b→a 8、c→t 2、s→a 8、s→t 9。從 s 到 t 的最大流是多少？
== 12
---
用擴增路徑一直推流，直到殘餘網路找不到 s 到 t 的路。最後從 s 在殘餘網路走得到的點是 {a, b, s}，對應的割切斷 s→t(9)、a→t(3)，容量和 12，正好等於最大流（最大流最小割定理），可以用來驗算。



@q pb3-35 | al-prob#3 | 3 | 計算

== 0,1,2,0,1,2,3,3,3
---

@q dc2-33 | al-dc#2 | 2 | 計算
Karatsuba 算 $9686\times5961$：切成 $a=96,b=86$、$c=59,d=61$。三次乘法 $ac$、$bd$、$(a+b)(c+d)$ 各是多少？中間項 $ad+bc$ 是多少？
== 5664,5246,21840,10930
?? ac,bd,(a+b)(c+d),ad+bc
---
$ac=96\times59=5664$、$bd=86\times61=5246$、$(a+b)(c+d)=182\times120=21840$。$ad+bc=(a+b)(c+d)-ac-bd=10930$。結果 $5664\times10^4+10930\times10^2+5246=57738246$。只用 3 次乘法（不是 4 次），遞迴下去得到 $O(n^{\lg3})$。

---
$ac=72\times97=6984$、$bd=78\times31=2418$、$(a+b)(c+d)=150\times128=19200$。$ad+bc=(a+b)(c+d)-ac-bd=9798$。結果 $6984\times10^4+9798\times10^2+2418=70822218$。只用 3 次乘法（不是 4 次），遞迴下去得到 $O(n^{\lg3})$。



@q sp4-32 | al-sp#4 | 3 | 計算
Johnson 演算法：圖的邊 a→c 5、a→d -1、b→a -1、b→d -1、c→b 4、d→c 0。加一個新點 $s$ 連到每個點（權重 0），用 Bellman-Ford 算 $h(x)=\delta(s,x)$。$h(a),h(b),h(c),h(d)$ 依序是？重新加權後 b→a 的權重 $\hat w=w+h(b)-h(a)$ 是？
== -1,0,-2,-2,0
?? h(a),h(b),h(c),h(d),新權重
---
從 $s$ 出發每個點一開始都是 0（直接走權重 0 的邊），再沿負邊往下壓：$h$＝(-1, 0, -2, -2)。b→a：$-1+(0)-(-1)=0$。所有新權重都 $\ge0$（三角不等式保證），之後每個點跑 Dijkstra；任一條路徑的長度只差 $h(\text{起點})-h(\text{終點})$，最短路徑不變。

@q dc5-30 | al-dc#5 | 3 | 計算
多項式 $A(x)=-1+x+4x^3$ 在 4 個 4 次單位根 $1,\ i,\ -1,\ -i$ 的值（也就是 DFT $y_0,y_1,y_2,y_3$）各是多少？（寫成 $a+bi$，例如 `2-3i`；純實數寫整數）
== 4,-1-3i,-6,-1+3i
?? y0,y1,y2,y3
---
$\omega=i$，$y_k=A(i^k)$。$y_0=A(1)=4$；$y_1=A(i)$：$i^2=-1,i^3=-i$，得 -1-3i；$y_2=A(-1)=-6$；$y_3=A(-i)=-1+3i$（實係數時 $y_3$ 是 $y_1$ 的共軛）。FFT 用偶次項、奇次項分開：$A(x)=A_e(x^2)+xA_o(x^2)$，只要算 $A_e,A_o$ 在 $\pm1$ 的值。

@q pb2-32 | al-prob#2 | 2 | 計算
某個 Monte Carlo 演算法只有單邊錯誤：答「是」一定對，答「否」時有最多 $1/4$ 的機率答錯。獨立重跑 $t$ 次、只要有一次答「是」就答是。要讓答錯機率 $\le10^{-4}$，$t$ 最少是多少？
== 7
---
$t$ 次都錯的機率 $\le(1/4)^t$。要 $4^t\ge10^{4}$：$t\ge4/\log_{10}4\approx6.64$，取 $t=7$（$4^{7}=16384$）。錯誤率隨次數**指數**下降，所以重跑幾十次就能讓錯誤小到可以忽略。

@q sp3-30 | al-sp#3 | 3 | 追蹤
Bellman-Ford，每一輪照固定順序鬆弛 d→a(3)、a→c(0)、s→d(0)、c→d(2)、a→b(3)、s→a(-2)、c→b(3)；某一輪完全沒有更新就停。最後 $a,b,c,d$ 的距離是？總共跑了幾輪（含最後那輪沒更新的）？
== -2,1,-2,0,3
?? a,b,c,d,輪數
---
第 1 輪後 a=-2, b=∞, c=∞, d=0；第 2 輪後 a=-2, b=1, c=-2, d=0；第 3 輪後 a=-2, b=1, c=-2, d=0。最終距離 a=-2, b=1, c=-2, d=0。最壞要 $V-1=4$ 輪才保證收斂，但順序剛好時可以提早停；加上「整輪沒更新就停」能省很多時間。

@q pb1-37 | al-prob#1 | 2 | 陷阱
10 個人把帽子丟進箱子，再每人隨機拿一頂（每種分法機率相同）。拿回自己帽子的人，期望有幾個？
[x] 1
[ ] 5
[ ] $1/10$
[ ] 0
---
第 $i$ 人拿到自己帽子的機率 $1/10$，設指示變數 $X_i$，$E[X]=\sum E[X_i]=10\times\frac1{10}=1$——和人數無關。各 $X_i$ 並不獨立，但期望值的線性性不需要獨立。


@q pb1-38 | al-prob#1 | 2 | 計算
把 1～6 隨機打亂（每種排列機率相同）。「$i<j$ 但 $a_i>a_j$」的位置對，期望有幾對是多少？（可用分數）
== 15/2
---
每一對位置 $(i,j)$ 設指示變數，兩個數誰大誰小各一半機率，$E=1/2$。共 $\binom{6}{2}=15$ 對，期望 $15\times\frac12=15/2$。插入排序的交換次數就是逆序數，所以它的平均情況也是 $\Theta(n^2)$。


@q pb1-39 | al-prob#1 | 2 | 陷阱
Six people put their hats in a box, and each then takes one at random, with every assignment equally likely. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Each person gets their own hat back with probability $\frac16$.
[x] The expected number of people who get their own hat back is 1.
[x] With 100 people, the expected number would still be 1.
[x] The events for different people are not independent.
[ ] The expected number is $\frac16$, which is the probability that any single person gets their own hat.
---
第 $i$ 人拿對的機率「$\frac16$」，6 個指示變數加起來「期望 1」，一般 $n$ 人是 $n\cdot\frac1n$，「人數變多也還是 1」。前面的人拿走誰的帽子會影響後面的人，「事件不獨立」，但線性不需要獨立。$\frac16$ 是單人的機率，不是總數。


@q pb1-40 | al-prob#1 | 3 | 計算

== 0.05
---
第 $j$ 個箱子是空的機率 $(1-\frac1{3})^{10}$。對每個箱子設指示變數再相加：$E=3\times(\frac{2}{3})^{10}\approx0.05$。同樣的想法可以算雜湊表裡空格的期望數。

@q rc4-33 | al-recurrence#4 | 3 | 計算
Let $T(n)=2T(\sqrt n)+1$, $T(2)=1$, with $n=2^{2^k}$. Count two recursive calls even when their inputs are equal; there is no saved-result reuse. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] With $m=\lg n$, the transformed recurrence is $S(m)=2S(m/2)+1$.
[x] At n equal to 65536 the full recursive expansion has 16 base calls.
[x] The solution is $\Theta(\lg n)$.
[ ] Equal child inputs allow one child call to be ignored under the stated evaluation rule.
[ ] The recursion tree has only four base calls at n equal to 65536 because its depth is four.
---
